### 249 subject events

- **1915.05** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Josef Bauer，楼里的Anna Moser正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Josef Bauer把裹着布条的Klara往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Josefine Hofer朝Klara招手。Klara挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Klara把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1918.03** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-win` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷子窄，两个孩子并排推着铁环，铁环撞在一起叮当作响。Klara的钩子先绕过最后一块界石。Mitzi Lechner不服，说再比一次，手却已经伸进口袋，摸出三颗彩色弹珠。Klara把弹珠举到眼前看，Mitzi Lechner在旁边踢墙，踢得鞋尖发白。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.05; skill_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Grete Bauer家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Klara递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Grete Bauer端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Klara把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1919.11** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Klara在洗衣房的长凳上睡着，醒来时喉咙像塞了棉花。Josef Bauer把最后的干净布巾浸了凉水，敷在Klara额头上，整夜换了七八回。第四天Klara能坐起来喝粥，下嘴唇脱下一小片皮，Josef Bauer用指尖替Klara拈掉。
  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  巷口的喊声第二次传来时，Klara正用膝盖压住木盆边沿，给Rosa Bauer挡穿堂风。Rosa Bauer在旧布底下翻了个身，Klara立刻停住，等呼吸又匀了才敢动。Josef Bauer推门进来，先看见盆里睡着的Rosa Bauer，再看见Klara冻红的脚背，把灶上留的汤热了热。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000573:child; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Klara的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Klara在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Luise Lechner从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Klara。Klara把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Klara后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Klara没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Klara用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1926.01** `r14-edu.tutoring-sibling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/own-marks-slipped` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, tutoring, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Klara每晚给Rosa Bauer讲题，自己的作业只能留到深夜。期中考试，Klara的名次掉了十几位，Maria Kral 2在家长会上点名。回家后，Klara看见Rosa Bauer趴在桌上睡着了，算术本还摊在胳膊下面，就没有叫醒。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999999; flag +neglected_own_studies; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1926.04** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/straight-to-factory` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  Katharina Lechner把Klara的名字从升学名单上划掉，说厂里正缺人手。家里没有人争，只把午饭里的半块面包塞给Klara。Klara进了纺织厂，工牌上写着“临时”，每天站十二个钟头，月底工钱比学徒多两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1926.06** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/stay-behind-the-curtain` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, reform, medical
  Klara把浴巾裹到下巴，说自己怕水。Mitzi在门外笑，说去年还见Klara从木塔上跳下去；Klara没有解释，只隔着板缝看Gustav Huber 2把水撩到肩上。回家时，Klara的泳衣还是干的，衣角被手攥得起了皱。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:104; flag +yf18_stayed_dry; mood -0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1927.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/walk-the-rail` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Klara翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Josef在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Klara翻回来时，Otto Berg把一支烟塞进Klara嘴里，手抖得点不着火。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 40 -> 50; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 15; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 5; reputation 0.02; flag +yf18_walked_bridge_rail; mood 0.05_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Klara在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Gustav Huber 2。这一次对方先点了头，Klara准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Klara才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

  Klara退烧第二天就回工厂，站到下午，眼前一阵阵发黑。回家后半夜又烧起来，她把湿毛巾搭在额头上，听见自己的心跳撞着枕头。第二天早上，Klara又去了，因为请假的扣款比药贵。
  _effects: cash 2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); condition; flag +returned_to_work_too_soon_

- **1930.04** `vienna-illness-household.child-fever/call-doctor` tags: health, family, home
  夜里Helene Bauer哭醒，Klara独自用湿布敷额，熬到街上有人扫雪才去请医生。医生进门时靴底带着雪泥，听完胸口说无大碍；那三克朗花得心疼，却换回一条稳下来的呼吸。
  _effects: cash -3 (支付医生诊费和药粉); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

  Klara把最后一块接榫敲进去，严丝合缝。Rosa Winter没有夸，只把成品翻过来看了看底，然后在自己名字旁边写下Klara的名字。

  Klara在巷口停了一下，没有走进去。第二天听说集会散了，没有人受伤。Klara说不清自己是松了一口气，还是有点后悔。
  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999; flag +avoided_assembly_

- **1931.01** `state_expire:stage-sovereign` tags: stage-sovereign, performance, theatre, pretend, status, irony, power, no-real-authority

- **1931.01** `r13-civ.midwife::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/doctor-called` tags: health, midwife, birth, neighbor, night, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa Lechner摸了半天肚子，忽然让Klara去街口叫医生，说胎位不对。Klara披着外套跑过两条街，医生来了以后只说了句“送医院”，又收了一次出诊费。天亮前，马车才把Anna Berg拉走。
  _effects: cash -5 (夜里敲接生婆的门); flag +complicated_birth_sent_to_hospital; relation colleague with person-rosa-lechner; knowledge “the midwife suspected a malpresentation before dawn”_

- **1931.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money
  抽屉一推就卡住，Klara用膝盖顶了一下才合严。上工路上经过药房，橱窗里摆着润肺糖浆的广告画，她别过头去。车间主任点名时，她应声比平时慢了一拍。
  _effects: condition; mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1931.04** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  第一趟过街口时车上的煤箱差点翻下来，Mitzi Kral用膝盖顶住，Klara把绳子又收了一道。第三趟回来，两人靠在门框上喘气，鞋底都是灰，省下的钱还揣在围裙里。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1931.05** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.fasching-dance/dance-cloakroom-scarf` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  衣帽间里两条围巾缠在一起，Luise Winter那条被别人的大衣压住了。Klara弯腰把围巾抽出来递过去，说了自己每周六在哪家铺子做工。看管费加一杯热茶正好一克朗；Luise Winter把围巾接过去，没说谢，只问了一句铺子几点关门。
  _effects: cash -1 (衣帽间看管费与一杯热茶); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

  Rosa Bauer差三分。回家路上，Rosa Bauer一句话也没说，进了门就把自己关在屋里。Klara把饭菜放在门口，敲了两下，没有回应。半夜，Klara听见屋里有人在翻书，翻到天亮。第二天清早，Rosa Bauer出来洗脸，眼睛肿着，问Klara能不能再教一年。补考费一克朗。
  _effects: cash -1 (弟弟妹妹的准考证); flag +sibling_failed_exam; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000573:child_

  铁桶磕在石沿上，水晃出来打湿了Klara的裙角。Luise Lechner的声音拔高，说轮到她就是轮到她。Klara咬着嘴没再喊，把盆往自己门前拖，湿石板上一道水痕。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.08** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1932.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 22; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 50; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +odd_job_scare_

  _effects: cash -5 (报名费不退，考试未过); mood -0.09999999999999998; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1935.01** `oj18-radio.broadcast::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/stayed-legal` tags: radio, broadcast, antenna, police, night, work, suspense, vienna
  Klara没有上屋顶，只把Mitzi Kral的天线图折好，塞进《无线电年鉴》里。第二天，Klara去给税务局修了一台官方收音机，顺手把音量调到最大；走廊里的人都听见了整点报时，没有人听见屋顶上的事。
  _effects: cash 2 (official radio repair fee); reputation 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; flag +stayed_legal; knowledge “税务局那台收音机的声音开得很大，走廊里的人都听见了整点报时。”_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:mb17-newcomer/first-year/puts-down-roots` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  一年过去，Klara能在菜市场用德语讲价，也知道哪家铺子肯赊半块黄油。房东仍叫他“新来的”，但楼下的面包师已经记住了他买哪一种面包，还会把前一天剩的那只小圆面包留出来。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 20 -> 24; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 7 -> 12; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 69 -> 67; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  一年过去，Klara已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Klara也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。

- **1935.04** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Josef Winter在午休时把本子摊在Klara的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Klara从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Josef Winter当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000157:child; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

- **1935.06** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  老工人凑近看那张表，说这纸比工资袋还薄。Klara把拇指蘸进印泥，按下去时听见工头说了句“下一个”。学徒在旁边数少掉的铜板，数到四十赫勒时不出声了。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.04999999999999999; flag +accepted_hours_reform_

- **1935.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Klara就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Alois Huber摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1936.01** `oj18-radio.repair::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/coil-in-the-cabinet` tags: radio, repair, craft, customer, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  Klara在机壳里摸到一只不属于收音机的线圈，还有一张卷起来的传单。Anna Moser 2说是朋友寄存的，让Klara别多问；第二天，两个穿便衣的人来问谁修过这台机器，Klara把工钱退回去，说只换了根线。
  _effects: state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:402; cash 1 (returned the repair fee); reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +suspicious_radio; knowledge “收音机里多了一只线圈和一张传单，{customer}说是朋友寄存的。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

  两人摸黑撬开一扇门，里面没有保险柜，只有背景布、三脚架和一池显影水。店主以为他们是卫生局派来查暗房通风的，殷勤地领着看排水口；Ferdinand Berg只好装模作样地记了几笔，Klara顺手把柜台上一顶帽子戴走。临走时，店主还问要不要写整改通知。
  _effects: cash 1 (卖掉顺走的帽子); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; condition; flag +burglary_mistaken_for_inspection; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

  Klara把婚纱熨得没有一道褶，交活时只收了料钱。Elisabeth Bauer在门口回头，说谢谢；Klara说祝您幸福。门关上后，她把剩下的缎带缠在手指上，缠得太紧，指节都白了。
  _effects: cash 2 (the wedding dress was paid for); mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +unspoken_fitting_desire_

  _effects: organization_found 第二区夜班出租合作社 (other); position 夜班司机 @taxi:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:007064; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:403; cash 2 (night taxi fares); knowledge “夜班只跑环城路，十一点以后不接去普拉特的车。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1937.10** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  表格有四栏，第四栏要写“工种及所属行会”。Klara问工友平时怎么称呼自己的活，工友靠在机器旁边说“就是上油”。办事员的印章是木柄的，按下去时咔的一声。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; skill_

  Rosa Bauer病倒的消息是邻居带来的。Klara赶到时，屋里只有一张床和一只药罐，孩子坐在门槛上。Klara留下来照顾了半个月，把自己的夜校报名费垫进了药钱。Rosa Bauer退烧那天，叫了Klara一声小名。药钱六克朗，是Klara垫的。
  _effects: cash -6 (多年后的一封家信); flag +cared_for_sick_sibling; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000573:child_

  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +faction_failed_to_form; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:oj18-workplace-legend/story-year/story-grows-teeth` tags: oj18-workplace-legend, work, legend, odd_job, rumor, identity, night, state
  故事传到第三个街区时，已经多出一只会说话的猫和两具没有头的尸体。Klara想纠正，发现自己越解释，听的人越兴奋；酒馆老板干脆把最离谱的版本写在黑板上，下面画了一个箭头：本店常客。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.legend 20 -> 28; state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.truth 55 -> 50; state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.toldCount 0 -> 2; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +legend_exaggerated_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Klara的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Klara想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 20 -> 12; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 40 -> 35; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1940.04** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.05999999999999994_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009077_

- **1940.05** `fa17-cabaret.debut::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/small-part` tags: art, performance, cabaret, debut, reputation, failure, theatre, vienna
  Emil Kral 2只给了Klara三句台词和一个端盘子的动作。排练时，Klara把那三句念得太用力，导演让重来，说“你不是在演一个端盘子的人，你是在演一个怕被看见的人”。首演后没有人记住Klara，但乐队指挥记住了Klara每次上台前都在后台数拍子。
  _effects: state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:556; cash 1 (小角色的排练补贴); role; mood -0.009999999999999898; flag +debut_small_part; state_add fa17-muse state:world-vienna-1912:557; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1940.08** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-scandal-helps` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  照片登出后，Klara收到十七封邀请函，都是请去讲“如何面对舆论”。一个工商业协会送来一笔“研究资助”，请Klara去讲一次闭门课；课酬刚好补上办公室这个月的缺口。
  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044; organization_funds; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:558; flag +scandal_turned_into_speaking_fee; knowledge “十七封邀请函里，有十二封问的是同一件事：能不能把丑闻讲成经验。”; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1940.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Klara没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000724:child_

- **1941.01** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/favor-refused` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Mitzi Hofer把一篮鸡蛋放在Klara家门口，说只要一个不用打卡的位子。Klara把鸡蛋拎回对方手里，说编制表不是自家的抽屉。两个人站在楼梯间，谁也没有再提这件事；第二天见面，还是点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000299:child; reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +refused_private_favor_

- **1941.01** `state_expire:journeyman` tags: journeyman, craft, work, skill, state, state_tick, state:journeyman
  Klara的「出师工匠」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove journeyman state:world-vienna-1912:230_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:wc17-master-craftsperson/workshop-year/commissions-arrive` tags: wc17-master-craftsperson, craft, work, master, workshop, ownership, reputation, state
  Klara的作坊又接了两单：一扇橡木门、一只修到一半的八音盒。Klara把工钱先付给学徒，自己留下最后一张钞票，用铅笔在账本背面记下欠料行的数目。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.backlog 1 -> 3; cash 2 (workshop commissions); reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  一年过去，Klara已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Klara也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 12 -> 17; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 30 -> 35; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 12; cash 1 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/a-year-of-work` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  又一年过去，Klara把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Klara把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 42 -> 46; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 12 -> 15; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 55 -> 53; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:fa17-muse/the-sitting-year/set-a-boundary` tags: fa17-muse, muse, art, desire, inspiration, dependence, vulnerability, beauty
  又一年过去，Klara学会了在进画室前先问清楚这次要坐多久、画完给谁看。Emil Kral 2嫌Klara变得难安排，却也不得不把每次画像的时间写在纸上；那张纸后来被Klara收进了自己的抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.autonomy 55 -> 60; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 10 -> 14; state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 35 -> 31; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:office-under-siege/pressure-year/held-on` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  质询会开了两个小时，Klara把每一笔签字都念了一遍。没有人被说服，但也没有人再追问；散会时，支持Klara的人比进门时多了两个。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.support 45 -> 50; state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 60 -> 56; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1941.02** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-witness-stays-quiet` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Berta Hofer在办事处说了Klara一整个下午的坏话，从欠债说到那场没人看见的火灾。旁边的人没有替Klara说一句话。几天后，Klara发现自己的名字从互助会的名单上被划掉了，Berta Hofer只说“我也要养家”。
  _effects: reputation -0.07999999999999996; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1941.02** `state_reaction:wc17-master-craftsperson/stolen-pattern` tags: wc17-master-craftsperson, craft, work, master, workshop, ownership, reputation, state
  有人拿走了Klara画在牛皮纸上的榫卯图样，几周后，同样的柜子出现在另一条街的橱窗里，标价更低。Klara没有去告，只在自家门框上重描了一遍印记。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.backlog 3 -> 1; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009870_

- **1941.06** `df18-mark.first-appearance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/burn-bird` tags: body, mark, omen, illness, rumor, supernatural, eerie, vienna
  印刷机的滚筒咬住Klara的袖口时，谁都以为只擦破了一层皮。伤口结痂后，却留下一只张开翅膀的鸟形烫痕。Maria Huber给了Klara两枚银币，说别去报工伤，厂里正查安全。Klara把钱收下，回到排字房，觉得那只鸟在袖子里发烫。
  _effects: cash 2 (工头给的封口费); state_add df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:601; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; flag +df18_mark_appeared_after_injury; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; flag +df18_mark_noticed; knowledge “我身上有一处说不清的痕迹，别人对它各有各的说法。”_

- **1941.12** `ml18-business.bottle-cap-patent::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/license-it-and-keep-the-patent` tags: business, patent, money, windfall, invention, comic, vienna, urban
  Klara没有卖断，而是和圣马克斯殡葬合作社签了按瓶抽成的许可：每卖出一千瓶，付两克朗。第一年只卖了四千瓶，Klara拿到八克朗，却保住了专利。Elisabeth Winter 2在许可书上盖了一个小章，说这种条款以后会值钱；Klara把章看了一会儿，没敢笑。
  _effects: cash 8 (first royalty payment on the bottle-cap patent); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:600; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; skill; flag +licensed_patent; mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009918_

- **1942.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/clean-audit` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Franz Lechner 2带了两只空箱子来，走的时候还是空的。Klara把三年的合同、收据和会议记录按月份排在地上，Franz Lechner 2抽查了十二份，每一份都能对上签字人；最后一句是“下次可以少准备一点”。
  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044; organization_funds; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +passed_audit; knowledge “审计抽查了十二份合同，每一份都能对上签字人。”_

- **1942.01** `r3-war.resistance-courier::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/carried-message-in-tire` tags: war, occupation, resistance, courier, checkpoint, vienna, urban, crisis
  Klara把纸条卷进自行车内胎，打足气，骑过检查站时还按了两下车铃。Elisabeth Bauer伸手拦车，问后座绑的什么；Klara说是给修女送的面粉。Elisabeth Bauer捏了捏轮胎，手指沾了一点黑油，挥手放行。骑出半里，Klara才发现后背全湿了。
  _effects: flag +carried_message_in_tire; role; knowledge “the checkpoint does not inspect tire pressure”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

  画室的门一关，时间就又变成Emil Kral 2的。Klara坐在同一把椅子上，听见窗外电车经过，数到第七次才被允许动一下；回家路上，Klara发现自己的围巾上沾着一股松节油味，洗了两次也没掉。

  又过了一年，Klara学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Klara的袖子也换了更长的款式。

- **1942.02** `ds17-courtship.parlour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-second-sunday` tags: love, courtship, intimacy, secret, class, secret_intimacy, vienna, urban
  Klara在约瑟夫城租下一间带钢琴的会客室，每小时一克朗，女房东把钥匙拴在一截蓝线上。Gustav Huber 2来时带着一束用报纸包好的紫罗兰，Anna Moser 2坐在窗边打毛线，偶尔抬头看一眼钟。第三个星期日，Anna Moser 2说自己要去楼下买咖啡，把门留了半掩。
  _effects: state_add forbidden-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:639; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; cash -1 (the parlour, the coffee and a bunch of violets); mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +rented_a_parlour; flag +secret_attachment_active_

- **1943.01** `initiative:found_process:org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:006032`
  权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。

- **1943.01** `historical_phase`
  历史进程《权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。
  _effects: knowledge “我亲历或知晓「权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招”; knowledge “我亲历或知晓「权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011457_

- **1943.05** `p9-history-union-political.reform-meeting/reform-hand-to-union` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  Klara把请愿书递给工会代表，自己退到后排靠柱子的位置。Otto Lechner从旁边递来一支短铅笔，让Klara把台上说的数目记在请柬背面，回家再誊清楚。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1943.07** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  Klara和Berta Huber在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Elisabeth Winter 2没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室); reputation 0.06000000000000005; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:011080_

- **1943.07** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011080_

- **1944.01** `r15-wish.power-offer::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/puppet-office` tags: politics, power, wish, office, status, vienna, urban, crisis
  报纸把Klara称作“新任主席”，照片很大，办公室很漂亮。真正的决定在另一间屋里做出。Klara每天签字，签的都是别人写好的东西。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation 0.07999999999999996; position 名誉主席 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:006032_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:fa17-muse/the-sitting-year/absorbed-again` tags: fa17-muse, muse, art, desire, inspiration, dependence, vulnerability, beauty
  画室的门一关，时间就又变成Emil Kral 2的。Klara坐在同一把椅子上，听见窗外电车经过，数到第七次才被允许动一下；回家路上，Klara发现自己的围巾上沾着一股松节油味，洗了两次也没掉。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 23 -> 29; state_parameter fa17-muse.inspiration 52 -> 54; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 34 -> 32; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  入冬后，Klara在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Klara低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 47 -> 57; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 22 -> 28; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed curse -> curse; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara为Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreich出了力：整理名单、传递消息或替同伴作证，名字被记进参与记录。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1933 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015000000000000013_

- **1944.03** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把通知念给Mitzi听，Mitzi摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Klara去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1944.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Alois Winter从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000154:child_

- **1944.12** `hs18-radio.rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/switch-it-off` tags: radio, blackout, rumor, panic, neighbor, politics, vienna, urban
  Klara伸手关掉收音机，说电池要留给夜里的急救。Otto Berg 2不同意，两人在黑暗里摸到同一只旋钮。最后谁也没有再开；楼道里只剩邻居的怀表在走，每一下都像在催人下楼。
  _effects: state_add hs18-blackout-rumor state:world-vienna-1912:697; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +silenced_radio_during_blackout_

- **1944.12** `state_reaction:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-public` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  Klara在人群里挽了一下袖子，第二天就有人来问那块印子是不是“会动的”。Klara说不会，问的人却已经把自己的解释说完了。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 39 -> 47; mood -0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011694_

- **1945.01** `r15-wish.faction-price::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/grew` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, intrigue, office, vienna, urban
  俱乐部从十一人变成四十人。Klara仍然坐主席的位置，但每次开会前都要先和Katharina Stein对一遍名单。有人开始叫Klara“主席”，也有人只在需要签字时才来。
  _effects: reputation 0.0249999999999998; knowledge “俱乐部扩大后，真正的权力不在主席的椅子上，而在会前对名单的修改。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011080_

- **1945.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Alois Winter抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Klara把床单对折，腾出一半，Alois Winter用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000154:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011696_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Klara躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Klara去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1945.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Klara难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Grete 2；Klara一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Otto Huber 2把钟表送进当铺。Klara落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:745_

- **1945.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/blocked-the-permit` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Klara把一份扩建许可证压在抽屉最下面，理由是排水和消防通道不合规。申请人Ferdinand Huber 2在走廊里等到下班，最后只拿到一张写着“补件”的纸条。Klara知道这份许可明年还会再来，但今年不会从自己的桌上出去。
  _effects: skill; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; flag +blocked_permit; knowledge “扩建申请的排水图与消防通道不符合现行规定。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

  冬天来得早，煤价涨了，主顾却要压价。Klara守着冷掉的胶锅，把最后一块好木料留给一单可能退掉的活；账本上的欠款又添了一行。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.debt 8 -> 11; cash -1 (workshop winter costs（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/a-year-of-work` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  又一年过去，Klara把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Klara把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 50 -> 54; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 18 -> 21; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 51 -> 49; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

  又过了一年，Klara学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Klara的袖子也换了更长的款式。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 57 -> 51; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 28 -> 24; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1945.05** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.buy-coal` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  炉门一开，黑烟先扑出来，Klara眯着眼把纸团往里塞。火苗舔着碎煤慢慢亮起来，Luise Hofer隔着门喊当心一氧化碳。水壶开始咕噜作响，屋里才有点热乎气。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); food_

- **1946.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/weathered` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  报纸在头版登了Klara和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Klara把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:771; flag +scandal_weathered; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1946.01** `r3-mig.passport-queue::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-passport-exchange/bought-a-place-in-line` tags: migration, papers, queue, bureaucracy, work, vienna, urban, postwar
  Otto Huber 2在队里替人占位，一个位子六克朗。Klara把钱塞过去，下午就站到了窗口前。Hedwig翻到出生地一栏，说拼写和档案不一致，要回原籍开证明。Otto Huber 2在门外等着分钱，听见这话，先把六克朗往口袋里按了按。
  _effects: cash -6 (雨里的护照队（其中6克朗记入债务）); flag +bought_queue_place; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; knowledge “a queue place costs six crowns”_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:fa17-muse/the-sitting-year/set-a-boundary` tags: fa17-muse, muse, art, desire, inspiration, dependence, vulnerability, beauty
  又一年过去，Klara学会了在进画室前先问清楚这次要坐多久、画完给谁看。Emil Kral 2嫌Klara变得难安排，却也不得不把每次画像的时间写在纸上；那张纸后来被Klara收进了自己的抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.autonomy 77 -> 82; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 30 -> 34; state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 35 -> 31; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  又过了一年，Klara学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Klara的袖子也换了更长的款式。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 51 -> 45; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 24 -> 20; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.01** `kernel-family.inheritance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/inheritance.older-will` tags: task, task:family, family, inheritance, siblings, estate, law, vienna
  Elisabeth Bauer从行李里掏出另一张更早的遗嘱，说工具和存款都该归长子。Elisabeth Winter 2把两张纸并排放好，说私人纠纷要去法院；诉讼费三十克朗，Klara拿不出。遗产在办事处冻结了半年，Klara靠替人浆洗撑过冬天。
  _effects: cash -3 (copies, stamps and travel to the inheritance office（其中3克朗记入债务）); relation rival with person-elisabeth-bauer; role; flag +inheritance_frozen; knowledge “遗产诉讼费要三十克朗，办事处不受理私人纠纷。”_

- **1946.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Otto Huber 2把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Klara去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中1克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

- **1946.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Klara嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Klara用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-mitzi-lechner; reputation 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 30 -> 40; state_parameter office-under-siege.support 60 -> 54; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.01** `r13-civ.quarantine::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/broke-quarantine` tags: health, quarantine, epidemic, neighbor, confinement, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara等到夜里，从后院的柴门溜出去上工。车间里没有人问，机器照常转，只有换班的人往旁边让了半步。回家时白线还在，Klara跨过去，鞋底带回一层湿泥。
  _effects: cash 3 (门框上的石灰线); reputation -0.09999999999999998; flag +broke_quarantine; knowledge “the workshop does not ask why a quarantined worker returned”_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; reputation -0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/flat-allocated` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, household
  等了三年，住房局终于给Klara一张分配单：利奥波德城一栋战后修复的公寓，两间房，走廊和水龙头仍与隔壁共用。Katharina Stein帮着把床架抬上三楼，墙上的弹孔用石灰草草抹过。
  _effects: residence; cash -10 (deposit and haulage); mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; role_

  投票结果是十九比十七。Klara把印章、钥匙和一本写满批注的账本放进纸箱，最后一圈走廊走得比平时慢。Franz Lechner 2在门口等着接手，手里已经拿着新的编制表；表上第一个被划掉的名字是Klara的秘书。
  _effects: position_end 夜班司机 @taxi:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005017; reputation -0.050000000000000044; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; flag +removed_by_vote; knowledge “新编制表第一个划掉的是我的秘书。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-135_

  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.040000000000000036; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +signed_balanced_budget; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; condition_

  你们站在当铺招牌下面，风从巷口灌进来。邻居说他婆娘这个月也要买煤，但两克朗还能匀出来。你把表给他看了一眼，说这表不能当，是爹留下的。他点点头，没再问。
  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001288:child_

  _effects: cash 4 (过期的当票); condition; flag +sold_pawn_ticket_to_neighbor; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002799:child; knowledge “a pawn ticket can be redeemed by anyone holding it”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120_

- **1949.11** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.cold-day` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  孩子放学回来搓着手说冷，Klara把仅有的半捧煤末压进炉底，火苗细得像灯芯。她没接Rosa Berg递来的煤块，只说等明天煤店开门。屋里哈气成雾，晚饭的土豆是凉的。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1950.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/private-household` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership, household
  Klara和Katharina Stein合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; household; mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1950.01** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  Klara和Berta Hofer在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Elisabeth Winter 2没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室（其中2克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.030000000000000138; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:015558_

  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.autonomy 87 -> 92; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 34 -> 38; state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 39 -> 35; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: condition; flag +kept_separate_money; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

- **1950.11** `bc18-enemy.accidental-help::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/warehouse-sinecure` tags: enemy, mistake, work, reputation, absurd, luck, social, vienna
  Mitzi Moser把Klara的名字推荐给码头仓库，说那里正缺一个“能扛两百斤的人”。Klara去了才知道，岗位是看管一只装满旧账本的暖房，工钱三克朗，主要工作是别让老鼠咬纸。Mitzi Moser听说后，在工会门口堵了三天，问这算哪门子惩罚。
  _effects: cash 3 (仓库暖房的看管工钱); role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; flag +enemy_recommended_a_sinecure; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-141_

  俱乐部从十一人变成四十人。Klara仍然坐主席的位置，但每次开会前都要先和Luise Bauer 2对一遍名单。有人开始叫Klara“主席”，也有人只在需要签字时才来。
  _effects: knowledge “俱乐部扩大后，真正的权力不在主席的椅子上，而在会前对名单的修改。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118_

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1951.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life

  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130; reputation 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130_

- **1951.07** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara没有立刻答话，先去敲Luise Weiss的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Luise Weiss提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Klara把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001288:child; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

  代表团没有带来任命书，只带来一套戏服：市庆游行需要一个“国王”，一天两场，管一顿饭。Klara穿上戏服时，围观的人鼓起掌来。掌声是真的，王冠是假的，晚饭也是真的。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:979; cash 2 (游行演出的酬劳); flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.010000000000000009_

  介绍所里那个Otto Huber说木牌上的地址多半过时了，不如自己去问。Klara走了四十分钟到洗衣房，院子里晾着床单。老板娘让她先熨一件试试，看完说留下吧，明天还这个时辰来。
  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill_

- **1952.09** `r3-crime.vigilante-justice::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/arrested-with-the-crowd` tags: crime, vigilante, violence, village, law, vienna, urban, postwar

  _effects: cash -8 (磨坊后面立着一根新木桩（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +arrested_after_vigilante_justice; role; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000154:child; knowledge “the constable charged the crowd, not the suspect”_

  Elisabeth Bauer把钱带来，却要Klara在借条上写明：明年开春若还不上，就替Elisabeth Bauer家的孩子交学费。Klara盯着那行字看了很久，还是按了手印。Elisabeth Bauer收起借条，说亲戚也要有凭据；Klara把钱揣进怀里，觉得比借来的还沉。
  _effects: cash 10 (信里只写了一个数目); condition; flag +accepted_conditional_family_loan; role; relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; knowledge “the loan is secured by a promise to pay school fees”_

- **1953.02** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.soup-kitchen/soup-got-scoop` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, relief, food, church
  风从教堂的拱廊里灌过来，队伍里没人说话，只听见罐子碰罐子的声音。Elisabeth Bauer排在前面，领完没走，站在墙根等Klara，把自己的面包掰了一半递过去。

- **1914.02** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/a-word-for-bread` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, rural, agriculture
  Mitzi指着桌上的面包说出一个短词，声音轻得像在试音。Rosa Lechner把面包掰下一角，泡进热水里喂给孩子；Mitzi吃完又指了一次，这次说得更清楚。厨房里的人都笑了，连灶上的水汽也像在往上冒。

  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Mitzi后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Mitzi没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Mitzi用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Mitzi站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Maria Kral在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Mitzi掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Mitzi才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral_

- **1921.01** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Mitzi跑到第二圈时鞋带松了，索性踩着鞋后跟跑。最后一圈，Mitzi从床单底下钻过去，先拍到墙。Josefine Hofer喘着气，把两颗弹珠拍进Mitzi手心。Mitzi低头才发现外套少了一颗扣子；窗口的Josefine Hofer扔下一根别针，说先别着。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Berta Stein把Mitzi拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Mitzi把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Mitzi蹲下把两包并排，按Berta Stein说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

  床单太长，Mitzi拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Grete Bauer弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Mitzi接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1922.04** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  土豆从围裙里滚出来，Mitzi蹲下去追，手心一松，三枚十克鲁泽的铜板叮叮当当掉在石板路上。两枚捡回来了，最后一枚滚进排水格栅，在铁条下转了个圈。Mitzi趴下去够，指甲刮在铁条上。回家Alois Lechner数完钱，脸色沉下来，Mitzi站在桌边不敢动。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.06_

  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

  客户刚把床单接过去，Grete Weiss就从楼梯口上来，盯着Mitzi看了两秒，说“你不是这家的”。Grete Weiss当场把工钱收回，说明天要去找Maria Lechner的雇主。Mitzi站在门口，手里还攥着空筐绳，回家一路上没吃东西，Maria Lechner问起来，只摇了摇头。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000078:child; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西)_

- **1926.02** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/signed-apprenticeship` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  毕业钟敲过三下，Katharina Lechner把成绩单按在讲台上，说Mitzi还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Mitzi签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; role; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

  多瑙河的浴棚按男女分开，木板缝里能看见对面的人影。Mitzi穿着借来的毛线泳衣，站在第三间门口不敢下水；Josef从水里冒出来，头发贴在额头上，说水不冷。Mitzi走进河里时，满脑子都是自己肋骨上那道疤。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:105; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

  Mitzi在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Mitzi用牙咬断，重新来。Alois Lechner坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Mitzi把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

  Maria Kral 2把一本拉丁文课本推到Mitzi面前，说第三十四页的译文错了。Mitzi翻到那一页，夹在里面的是一张油印纸，写着面粉配给和工厂检查的数字。放学后，Mitzi把纸抄了一遍，塞进Emil的历史作业本；那天晚上，Mitzi第一次觉得自己住的街区有一张看不见的地图。

  工头说这个月赶印一批账簿，晚上加班每小时多给二十赫勒。Mitzi站在教室门口听完课程表，转身回厂里搬纸。Otto Lechner追出来说座位可以保留两周，Mitzi点点头，手已经摸上了作坊的门把，夜里机器的声音把课本的事盖了过去。

  Mitzi以为那是谁开的玩笑，把地图册举起来念给全班听。Friedrich Weiss问是谁写的；教室最后一排有人把书立起来挡住脸。放学后，Elisabeth Bauer在楼梯口等Mitzi，只说了一句“你念错了一个字”，就从他身边走过去。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:190; reputation -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1930.01** `p10-migration-tasks.ask-travel-money/aunt-private-money` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, loan, relative
  舅父在里屋翻账簿半天没出来，舅母趁空从围裙里掏出一卷私房钱，数出十克朗塞给他，说到了家乡先给她写封信。他把钱收下，出门时在门口站了一会儿，才把帽子戴正。
  _effects: cash 10 (舅母私下给的路费); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1930.02** `r15-ordinary.quiet-year::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/small-good-day` tags: ordinary, daily, family, leisure, peace, reform, medical, schooling
  这一年没有发生什么大事。Mitzi把窗台擦干净，邻居送来一把樱桃，孩子在走廊里跑来跑去。晚上熄灯前，Mitzi想起一件很久以前的事，笑了一下。
  _effects: food; mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +ordinary_good_year_

- **1930.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Otto Berg 2嫌Mitzi的床单滴水滴到Otto Berg 2家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Mitzi下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Mitzi把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Otto Berg 2那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_

- **1930.10** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Josefine Hofer合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1931.01** `bc18-enemy.accidental-help::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/wrong-coal-delivery` tags: enemy, mistake, work, reputation, absurd, luck, social, reform
  Franz Lechner 2把二十袋“劣质煤”送到Mitzi门口，货到付款，想看Mitzi拿不出钱。卸货时，煤块黑得发亮，是上好的无烟煤；Mitzi付了钱，转手卖给面包房，净赚五克朗。Franz Lechner 2来打听煤的去向，Mitzi说已经烧了，还谢谢他记得自己怕冷。
  _effects: cash 5 (转卖无烟煤的差价); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; condition; flag +enemy_coal_was_anthracite; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

  动身前一天，城里的作坊捎话让他去试工。他把车票退掉，扣了些手续费，拎着工具袋去了作坊，在车床边站了一整天。哥哥的信还压在枕头下，他回了一封，说再等等看。
  _effects: cash -1 (退票扣除的手续费); mood 0.03999999999999998; relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; knowledge “城里那家作坊开工，试工一天给两克朗五十赫勒，转正看手艺”_

  Mitzi喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Friedrich Weiss在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.09** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/hire-care` tags: health, family, home

  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用（其中1克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (看护妇料理后伤势好转)_

  _effects: cash 4 (商行聘书的预付薪金); reputation 0.04999999999999999; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:378; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; condition; flag +enemys_letter_became_an_advert; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  炉子封小了，屋里凉得快，Mitzi把大衣盖在被子上。安娜收碗时看见窗台上一排药瓶，没说什么，第二天多带了半块黑面包。楼下的管道工敲了一下午铁皮，咳嗽声混在里面听不真切。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1935.12** `ml18-luck.strongbox-in-the-wall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-notes-are-still-good` tags: money, luck, housing, windfall, inflation, ownership, vienna, urban
  泥瓦匠敲开卧室的旧墙，掉出一只锈死的铁盒；里面是一叠旧纸币、一枚银扣和一张当票。Maria Huber 2闻声上楼，说墙是他家的，盒子也该归他。Mitzi把当票翻过来，看见上面写着自己外祖母的名字，两个人都安静了一会儿。
  _effects: cash 45 (old banknotes found in the wall); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:379; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; flag +found_strongbox; knowledge “铁盒里的当票写着外祖母的名字，纸币是很多年前的旧版。”; mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1936.01** `ml18-tax.audit-of-the-windfall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/pay-and-regularize` tags: tax, legal, money, windfall, bureaucracy, comic, vienna, urban
  Friedrich Hofer把黑皮本翻到Mitzi那一页，说邮局储蓄的利息没有报，数目不大，但漏了就是漏了。Rosa Berg在旁边把计算尺推来推去，最后算出一个八克朗的数目。Mitzi当天去缴了款，把收据贴在账本最后一页；回家的路上，账本第一次没有在口袋里折着。
  _effects: cash -8 (back taxes and a small fine on the windfall's interest（其中8克朗记入债务）); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 40 -> 32; reputation 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +taxes_paid; knowledge “邮局储蓄的利息也要报税，漏报的八克朗已经补齐。”; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Mitzi仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Mitzi学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 20 -> 27; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 2 -> 3; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.009999999999999995; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Mitzi把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Mitzi来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 4; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.box-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Mitzi咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Mitzi手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Hedwig等在门口，问值不值，Mitzi只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价); mood 0.07999999999999996; reputation 0.020000000000000004; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1936.04** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Mitzi点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Luise Berg量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.04** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Mitzi的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Mitzi家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007071_

  Mitzi把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Emil Huber合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

  报纸的社会版把Mitzi家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public true -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 4 -> 6; reputation -0.020000000000000004; flag +windfall_went_public_

  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Mitzi把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 32 -> 27; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi把夜里咳醒的次数写在一张包装纸上，Emil Kral 3扫了一眼，说是心事太重，给了几包安神粉。走廊尽头的护士压低声音，说住院部还有空床，但要医生签字。Mitzi把包装纸折回去，没再问。
  _effects: cash -1 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +clinic_dismissed_as_nerves; knowledge “the clinic doctor refused a hospital referral”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

- **1937.02** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/queue-until-noon` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Mitzi在诊所门口从五点半站到十一点，膝盖发僵，终于用最后四十赫勒付了挂号费。门房撕下转诊单，叫Mitzi去综合医院排队，药费还得另算；队伍拐过街角时天还黑着，Mitzi数着前面二十三个后脑勺，记住了综合医院药房下午才开窗口。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (诊所挂号费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); knowledge “综合医院的药房比街角药房便宜，但要排到下午”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1937.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Mitzi听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Hermann Winter 2扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用（其中3克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

  _effects: reputation -0.039999999999999994; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Elisabeth Bauer在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Mitzi排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition_

  Mitzi傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Hedwig在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Hedwig敲开Mitzi的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: flag +petition_refused; reputation 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; cash -0.5 (重抄签名纸的纸笔钱（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); knowledge “请愿书要按街道分页，窗口换班时前一班可能没有交接”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  Otto Berg 2在办事处说了Mitzi一整个下午的坏话，从欠债说到那场没人看见的火灾。旁边的人没有替Mitzi说一句话。几天后，Mitzi发现自己的名字从互助会的名单上被划掉了，Otto Berg 2只说“我也要养家”。
  _effects: reputation -0.07999999999999999; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008157_

  Mitzi在楼梯口拦住Anna Weiss，问是不是家里来了亲戚。Anna Weiss说是远房表弟，病了，怕传染。Mitzi注意到Anna Weiss说“表弟”时，手指一直捻着围裙边，地下室的灯却在他身后亮了一整夜。

  _effects: cash -1 (公墓新翻的土); condition; flag +visited_a_grave_with_neighbour; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

  Mitzi拿着锡罐在市场和厂门口转了一天。肉铺老板放了两枚克朗，面包房给了一袋隔夜面包，Otto Berg 2把准备交房租的钱数出三分之一。天黑时锡罐里有四十多克朗，Mitzi把每一笔都写在包装纸上，连半赫勒也没漏。

  Mitzi没有立刻答话，先去敲Anton的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Anton提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Mitzi把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。

  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 29 -> 26; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.proof 27 -> 29; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: cash -1 (补助金窗口的第二张表（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.029999999999999916; flag +work13_benefit_rejected_missing_stamp; knowledge “the final three months of employment were never stamped”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

  柜台上摊着两张工资袋，一张油墨新，一张已经磨毛了边。Mitzi把两袋并排放，掌柜伸出食指把旧的拨到一边，说“你管好自己的”。师兄在窗口看街对面修鞋摊，一直没回头。
  _effects: reputation -0.03999999999999998; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; skill_

  Mitzi没去事务所，只在洗衣房打听更便宜的床位。有人提到利奥波德城另一条街上有一间合住的屋子，一个月少一克朗半，只是要跟三个人共用一间。Mitzi去看了两趟，量了窗子和床的位置，回来把箱子底下的旧报纸铺开算账。
  _effects: flag +looking_for_cheaper_housing; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1941.05** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Otto Weiss 2点了牛奶咖啡，Mitzi要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡（其中0.12克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

- **1942.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-seal-was-used` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Friedrich Weiss用Mitzi留在抽屉里的印章签了一份采购单，把库房里最后一批煤卖给了城外的砖厂。Mitzi发现时，账已经做平，签字栏里是Mitzi自己的名字。Friedrich Weiss说只是替Mitzi省了麻烦，还把那支用旧的印泥放回了原处。
  _effects: cash -3 (为平账先垫出去的钱（其中3克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.09999999999999998; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; condition; flag +seal_used_by_trusted_hand_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:sm18-town-secret/the-secret-keeps/a-sentence-slips` tags: sm18-town-secret, secret, neighborhood, rumor, knowledge, risk, obligation, mystery
  Mitzi本来只想说一句“那家人最近不太容易”，话到嘴边却带出了地下室。听的人没有追问，只把杯子放下；第二天，楼道里多了几道从门口扫到楼梯口的目光。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 26 -> 33; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.danger 26 -> 34; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/chronic-cough` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi在诊所走廊的第三张长凳上坐到中午，膝上压着挂号纸和一块湿手帕。Luise Winter 2把听诊器贴上来不到半分钟，说是潮气和烟，开了褐色止咳糖浆，又让下周再来。药费从当天的菜钱里扣走，回家时篮子里只剩半棵卷心菜。
  _effects: cash -3 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +clinic_first_visit; knowledge “the clinic doctor called the cough chronic bronchitis”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

- **1942.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Karl Kraus嫌Mitzi的床单滴水滴到Karl Kraus家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Mitzi下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Mitzi把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Karl Kraus那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-karl-kraus; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_

- **1942.09** `r2-rural.barn-fire::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/lost-the-barn` tags: rural, fire, neighbor, loss, insurance, vienna, urban, crisis
  火从草料棚烧起来，等第二区夜班出租合作社的手压泵接上井水，谷仓只剩黑梁。Mitzi和Maria Lechner在灰里扒出半袋没烧透的麦子，袋口一碰就碎。Maria Lechner蹲在地上，把焦麦一粒粒捡进帽子。
  _effects: cash -18 (谷仓夜里的红光（其中18克朗记入债务）); flag +lost_barn_to_fire; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000078:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

  Mitzi把旧练习本翻到空白一页，写下母亲、自己和Mitzi Lechner的名字。十四克朗的药费单，Mitzi和Mitzi Lechner各摊五克朗，余下四克朗从母亲的养老金里扣；Mitzi把五克朗硬币一枚枚摞在桌沿，Mitzi Lechner盯着那一栏看了半天，也从围裙口袋里摸出钱来。
  _effects: cash -5 (分摊母亲药费中自己的一份（其中5克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner_

- **1943.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/weathered` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  报纸在头版登了Mitzi和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Mitzi把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:666; flag +scandal_weathered; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; flag +office_under_siege_

  _effects: historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1933 as participant; knowledge “我参加了Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SD。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.11** `r3-crime.petty-theft-arrest::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::wartime-ration-bread/extra-charge-at-the-station` tags: crime, police, poverty, law, shop, vienna, urban, crisis
  到了警局，失窃清单上忽然多出一只银怀表，Elisabeth Bauer说是在Mitzi的篮子底下找到的。Mitzi说那篮子从早上就没离手，Alois Hofer低头擦柜台，Anton被叫去另一间屋子。值班的人把“盗窃”改成“加重盗窃”，让她按手印。
  _effects: cash -6 (后巷里数面包的手（其中6克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +overcharged_after_petty_theft; role; relation rival with person-elisabeth-bauer; knowledge “the silver watch was added to the list after the arrest”_

  Mitzi只在菜摊前说了半句“那家人地下室里”，第二天整条街都在传Mitzi Lechner杀了人。Rosa Berg一边称土豆一边问尸体埋在哪；Mitzi说不是这么回事，Rosa Berg已经把这话当成了新的版本。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 30 -> 42; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.danger 34 -> 44; reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; knowledge “街上正在传邻居杀了人，这不是真的”; flag +started_a_false_rumor_

  _effects: cash -0.3 (还价后买下旧杯子（其中0.3克朗记入债务）); skill_

  你数出四十赫勒搁在秤盘上，说剩下的一克朗用那张旧折叠桌抵。老板撇了撇嘴，让伙计跟你上楼搬桌子。桌子腿有点晃，但木料还结实。邻居在楼道里搭了把手，账本上这一页只写了一行，没写利息。

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; food; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  _effects: death (暴力)_

- **1917.11** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-lose` tags: childhood, street, social, home

  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.04; condition_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work

  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

  Josef在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Mitzi。这一次对方先点了头，Josef准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Josef才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

  Josef每晚给Helene Bauer讲题，自己的作业只能留到深夜。期中考试，Josef的名次掉了十几位，Friedrich Weiss在家长会上点名。回家后，Josef看见Helene Bauer趴在桌上睡着了，算术本还摊在胳膊下面，就没有叫醒。

  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-luise-kral_

  Josef在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Mitzi三步远的地方拐进面包店。Josef买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。

  _effects: relation partner with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03999999999999998; cash -4 (separate room rent)_

- **1931.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family

  _effects: reputation -0.04; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

  介绍所的柜台前还有几个人在等，Franz Lechner 4把一张印好的通知推过来，用铅笔在“星期三”下面画了一道。他把纸条折成四折，塞进上衣内袋。
  _effects: flag +seeking_work; knowledge “下星期三早上七点，另一家机器厂在职业介绍所登记招上油工”; mood -0.05999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1931.05** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Josef把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Emil Kral 2提着空碗上来时，只看见Josef把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1931.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Anna Moser 2抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Josef把床单对折，腾出一半，Anna Moser 2用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +wish:craft:pursuing_

  Josef醒后只当是晚饭太咸，翻个身又睡了。下午，地窖的水管爆开，煤和旧箱子泡在齐膝的水里。Hermann Winter 2帮着舀水，Josef一边舀一边想起梦里的水位，正好到第二个台阶。

- **1932.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Josef在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-josefine-hofer_

  Josef在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Marie Huber拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失)_

  放映到第十一本，胶片在片门里卷成一团。Josef剪掉烧焦的一截，发现后面接的是一卷没送审的新闻片：军靴踏过广场，镜头扫过一张张被按下去的脸。Friedrich Weiss在黑暗里说“接回正片”，Josef照做了。
  _effects: organization_found 小巷电影院 (household_firm); position 放映员 @cinema:person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005013; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:284; cash 1 (projectionist wages); skill; flag +cut_the_reel; knowledge “第十一本胶片里夹着没送审的新闻片，{projectionist}让我接回正片。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +gave_up_on_letter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.06 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

  Josef说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Gustav Moser没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; flag +funeral_society_left; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

  _effects: death (肺炎)_

- **1913.04** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/scalded-hand` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Josef Bauer提热水壶往木盆里兑水时壶底脱了焊，滚水溅在左手背上。她咬着牙把壶搁下，Emil被响声惊得大哭。手背起了水泡，接下来三天她只能用一只手洗尿布。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (左手被开水烫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1915.03** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Emil踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Josef Bauer把Emil抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Emil举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.03999999999999998 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.05; skill_

  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1923.02** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书页在桌肚里翻动时，Friedrich Weiss已经站在旁边。Emil的手还按在算术本上，书被抽走，书脊在讲台边磕了一声。Friedrich Weiss看了一眼折角，说“放学留下”，又拉开抽屉把书放进去。Emil盯着抽屉缝，整节课没听进去。
  _effects: mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1923.06** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Emil每天放学都在柜台前站一会儿，看玻璃板底下压着的烟画，顺手把歪了的报纸摞齐。Mitzi擦完柜台，从抽屉最里面摸出一张揉皱的风景卡，边角还沾着烟丝，塞过来时说了句“别挡着门”。Emil把卡夹进课本，第二天带了一张重复的来给Mitzi看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.02_

- **1925.01** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/keep-it-in-the-satchel` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Emil把手套塞进书包最里层，回家后放在枕头下面。第二天上学，Emil每隔一会儿就摸一下书包带，像在确认一件不能给家里看见的东西；手套上还留着电车木座的气味。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:82; flag +yf18_kept_glove; mood 0.02; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1925.10** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa Winter先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Emil每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.039999999999999994; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1926.01** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil在衣帽间堵住Josef，问那两张票怎么到了Elisabeth Bauer手里。Josef说Emil从来没敢开口，自己只是替Emil把话说了；Emil把票根揉成一团，塞进Josef的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 10; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 5 -> 0; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.02; mood -0.05_

- **1926.01** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000001_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002535_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Emil废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Emil在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Josef。这一次对方先点了头，Emil准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Emil才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 65 -> 70; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

- **1926.01** `r13-edu.music-lesson::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/muted-practice` tags: education, music, lesson, craft, culture, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil在阁楼练琴，楼下邻居上来敲门，说孩子要睡觉。Emil把弱音器夹上，声音闷得像蚊子。练到半夜，Emil索性放下琴，把谱子默写了一遍，第二天在课上凭记忆拉完，Maria Kral 2问Emil是不是没睡好。第二天Emil把弱音器取下来，换到更远的阁楼。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1926.07** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/give-up-for-overtime` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  工头说这个月赶印一批账簿，晚上加班每小时多给二十赫勒。Emil站在教室门口听完课程表，转身回厂里搬纸。Mitzi Stein追出来说座位可以保留两周，Emil点点头，手已经摸上了作坊的门把，夜里机器的声音把课本的事盖了过去。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1926.12** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/signed-apprenticeship` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  毕业钟敲过三下，Friedrich Weiss把成绩单按在讲台上，说Emil还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Emil签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; education apprenticeship_

- **1927.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/walk-the-rail` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Emil翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Mitzi在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Emil翻回来时，Elisabeth Bauer把一支烟塞进Emil嘴里，手抖得点不着火。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 70 -> 80; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 8 -> 23; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 15; reputation 0.02; flag +yf18_walked_bridge_rail; mood 0.04999999999999999_

  Emil的信寄出半年，没有回音。后来Katharina Lechner在一篇文章里引用了这封信，没有署名，只写一个读者说。Emil在报上读到自己的句子，先是高兴，随后发现文章里的意思和自己写的相反。

- **1928.01** `r13-edu.music-lesson::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/broken-string` tags: education, music, lesson, craft, culture, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil换把时用力过猛，弦轴崩了，琴弦抽在手背上。Mitzi Stein没有责备，只让Emil先学三个月空弦。Emil每天对着镜子拉同一个音，拉到最后，连邻居都不再敲墙，只是经过门口时会放慢脚步。第二天Emil照常来，手上缠着布。弦上还有血。
  _effects: cash -2 (借来的小提琴); skill_

- **1928.07** `yf18-friend.inkwell::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/name-the-friend` tags: exam, cheating, friendship, betrayal, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil说Elisabeth Bauer把答案抄在墨水瓶底，还答应分给自己一半。Katharina Lechner把Elisabeth Bauer的座位调到第一排；第二天，Emil的课桌里多了一张纸条，上面只有一行字：“你以后别走夜路。”Emil把纸条交给Katharina Lechner，事情从此不再是两个人的事。
  _effects: relation_end friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; reputation 0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_named_friend; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Emil在炉子前把信烧了，纸灰卷起来，像一片黑叶子。Josef第二天问有没有话要说，Emil说没有。两个人还是一起走了一段路，在面包店门口分开，谁也没有回头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +burned_the_schoolbook_letter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1929.01** `r13-edu.dropout-return::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/returned-to-school` tags: education, dropout, return, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil退学半年后，在工厂门口看见以前的同学背着书包走过。Emil回家翻出课本，书页已经发黄。第二天，Emil去找Alois Hofer，Alois Hofer让Emil先跟班旁听两周。Emil坐在最后一排，把落下的笔记一页页补回来。两个月后，Emil的座位从最后一排换到中间。
  _effects: flag +returned_to_school; education secondary_

- **1929.05** `r3-teen.first-love::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rejected` tags: love, courtship, school, tram, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil把叠成方块的纸条塞给Josef Winter，第二天在课桌里找回同一张纸，边角压平了，字没有被打开过。放学时Josef Winter从另一边走了，Emil坐在电车上，一直坐到终点站。
  _effects: mood -0.16000000000000003; flag +first_love_rejected; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000157:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

  Emil把那页地图撕下来，折成四折藏进袜筒。回家路上，Emil在煤气灯下把字读了三遍，又把纸塞进炉子；火苗舔到“多瑙河”三个字时，Emil伸手抢出来，指尖烫出一个泡。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:188; flag +yf18_burned_fingers; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1930.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Alois Lechner把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Emil去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-alois-lechner_

- **1930.01** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Berta Stein把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Emil把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Berta Stein低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; flag +rent_increase_accepted; health -0.019999999999999997 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase)_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/rehearsed-conversation` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Emil在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Elisabeth Bauer三步远的地方拐进面包店。Emil买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 50 -> 56; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 3; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Emil只喝了半杯就看见门口站着等位的老主顾，手里转着帽子。Emil把报纸叠好放回木架，起身让出窗边的位子，一杯 Melange 三十赫勒，侍者点头致意，没再收第二杯的钱。走出门时雨小了些，Emil站在檐下理了理衣领，觉得这半个下午也不算白来。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (一杯 Melange 的价钱); reputation 0.03; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1930.03** `mb17-border.permit-window::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-with-papers` tags: migration, border, papers, permit, station, official, belonging, reform
  火车在清晨进站，Franz Lechner 2把Emil的护照翻到最后一页，又看了看那张没有照片的出生纸。蓝章落下去，手续费和住址登记表一起被推到窗口；Emil把临时居留纸折成四折，塞进内衣口袋，走出站门时天还没有完全亮。
  _effects: cash -2 (the passport fee and the first week's rent); state_add mb17-newcomer state:world-vienna-1912:222; state_add mb17-remittance-web state:world-vienna-1912:223; role; flag +mb17_admitted_at_border; mood 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “临时居留纸要求在四十八小时内到区公所登记住址。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; flag +mb17_newcomer_active; flag +mb17_remittance_web_active_

  琴箱的螺丝松了，每转一圈都发出吱呀声，可圆舞曲还是圆舞曲。她靠着墙听完，看见三楼窗台有人用绳子吊下一只小篮子收钱，绳子在风里晃。她摸出铜板时，猴子已经蹲在她脚边了。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (给手摇风琴师的赏钱); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

  一个刚下火车的Hedwig站在门口，鞋上全是泥，说找不到住处。Klara让他先坐下，Emil把汤推过去，又把一间便宜床位的地址写在火柴盒上；那天长桌挤得坐不下，谁也没有先走。
