  _effects: cash -1 (更正材料的手续费和车钱); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:1024; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-161; condition; flag +two_living_files_one_death; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-161; flag +bureaucratic_ghost_

- **1953.01** `state_reaction:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/another-file-opened` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Hedwig刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Hedwig站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 15 -> 27; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 2 -> 3; flag +another_file_opened_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017626_

- **1953.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-ambulance` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  助理医生拿不准，叫了救护车。Hedwig被抬进医院，走廊里等了六个小时才排到床位；家里人后来算了一笔账，救护车比药贵。救护车在石板上颠，他抓着担架边，数着路灯。医院门口的担架已经排到街上。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; cash -15 (ambulance and hospital admission（其中15克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (admitted for observation and oxygen); role; knowledge “实习医生拿不准就会叫救护车；走廊等六个小时才有床，车费比药贵。”_

- **1953.04** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/keep-watch-borrow-elsewhere` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你们站在当铺招牌下面，风从巷口灌进来。邻居说他婆娘这个月也要买煤，但两克朗还能匀出来。你把表给他看了一眼，说这表不能当，是爹留下的。他点点头，没再问。
  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.04** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Gustav Huber追出来问怎么不领了，Hedwig说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Hedwig把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; food_

- **1954.01** `bc18-ghost.pension-window::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/posthumous-medal` tags: bureaucracy, pension, identity, legal, paperwork, absurd, poverty, vienna
  养老金办公室寄来一枚“忠诚遗属”铜章和三克朗，说这是对死者家属的最后抚恤。Hedwig不能亲自去领，只好让Elisabeth Winter 2代收；邻居把铜章别在自家外套上，回来时只字不提三克朗。Hedwig没有追问，因为那枚铜章若挂在自己胸前，实在不知道该怎么解释。
  _effects: cash -1 (代领材料的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 27 -> 35; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 3 -> 4; reputation 0.009999999999999995; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; condition; flag +neighbor_kept_the_medal_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Hedwig的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Hedwig收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Hedwig先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 40 -> 47; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 35 -> 45; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead_

- **1954.01** `r13-civ.soup-kitchen::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/served-three-hours` tags: social, charity, soup_kitchen, hunger, volunteer, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig站在汤锅后面，把勺子往碗沿上磕两下，再递给下一只手。队伍从门口拐到巷子里，有人端着碗蹲在墙根吃。收摊时，Berta Hofer把锅底最后一点汤刮给Hedwig，说今天够了。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +volunteered_at_soup_kitchen; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1954.07** `df18-curse.name-in-the-door::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/the-joke` tags: curse, name, door, omen, neighbor, dark, supernatural, vienna
  Hedwig挨家问过去，最后Elisabeth Winter 2承认是自己刻的，说只是开个玩笑，因为Hedwig最近总说运气不好。Hedwig看着那张笑脸，没有发火，只把门框上的字用砂纸磨掉。磨到最后，木头里露出另一行更旧的刻痕。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; knowledge “门框上的名字是邻居刻的，但木头里还有更旧的字。”; condition; flag +df18_old_name_under_door_

- **1954.09** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Hedwig在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Hedwig去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为环城大道写字楼管理处的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

- **1955.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-accusation-fails` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Winter 2在众人面前说Hedwig用诅咒害人。没有人接话，因为上个月Elisabeth Winter 2也用同样的话说过另一个人。事情没有闹大，但Hedwig发现，从此每次走进那间屋子，都有人先看Hedwig的手。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:1104; flag +accusation_echoed_

- **1955.01** `state_reaction:suspected-witchcraft/another-name` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  Elisabeth Winter 2在指控Hedwig的人面前添了一句话，说Hedwig曾经在夜里念过谁的名字。那句话没有证据，但登记册上又多了一行。Hedwig后来才知道，添这句话的人就是Elisabeth Winter 2。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 15 -> 25; reputation -0.03_

  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig听见墙里有一声很轻的响。第二天，Elisabeth Winter 2病倒了；没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: condition; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 2 -> 3; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Luise Huber把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Hedwig去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Katharina Weiss 2给Hedwig一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Hedwig把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金); health 0.05999999999999999 (住院处窗口的押金); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139_

- **1955.03** `r1-hc.blackmail::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/blackmail-police` tags: blackmail, secret, family, money, police, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig拿着信封去了警局。Rosa Hofer看完照片，先问Hedwig和照片里的女人是什么关系，又问孩子现在在哪；笔录做完，信封被收走，Berta Winter 2却再没出现过。回家时，Hedwig发现门缝里塞着一张同样的照片。
  _effects: cash -2 (信封里那张照片); reputation -0.05; flag +reported_blackmail; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-159; knowledge “the police keep the envelope and ask about the child first”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-159_

- **1955.07** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/tells-the-priest` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  Hedwig在告解室里把事情说了一遍，没有提名字。神父沉默了很久，只问Hedwig是否愿意把那张纸交出来。Hedwig说没有纸。走出教堂时，Hedwig发现街对面有人一直在等，手里拿着一本新的登记册。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:1105; reputation -0.020000000000000004_

- **1955.09** `hs18-cinema.newsreel::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/recognize-a-neighbor` tags: cinema, newsreel, neighbor, recognition, media, history, vienna, urban
  银幕上是城西的街口，镜头扫过一排晾衣绳。Hedwig认出绳下站着Elisabeth Winter 2，也认出旁边那个被两名便衣夹住的人。散场灯亮时，Elisabeth Winter 2还在笑，Hedwig却把围巾往上拉了拉。
  _effects: knowledge “新闻片里那个被便衣夹住的人，我认识。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

- **1956.01** `curse15-aftermath.the-inquiry::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/money-closes-the-file` tags: witchcraft, investigation, reputation, rumor, legal, aftermath, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Winter 2托人带话，说只要Hedwig出钱修好教堂的侧门，证词就可以当作没有写过。Hedwig把钱数了三遍，最后交出去。侧门修好了，Elisabeth Winter 2在门框上刻了一行小字，刻的是Hedwig的名字。
  _effects: cash -3 (修教堂侧门的封口钱); state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 25 -> 10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +paid_to_close_accusation_

- **1956.01** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, injury
  手推车每走十几步就要停一下，楼道里的门半开着，有人探头看热闹。Hedwig把照片铁盒交给Berta Winter 2抱着，自己低头把最后一箱锅具拖上楼。天擦黑时，新屋里才勉强归置出下脚的地方。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); health -0.05000000000000002 (三趟推车扛家具累得肩臂酸痛); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108_

- **1956.01** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  浆糊是隔夜的，刷上去时带着股酸味。Hedwig把纸四角按平，有人凑近念出“一九〇一年，欠三十四克朗”。办公室里的椅子腿不平，坐在上面的人一动就晃，可没人起身。
  _effects: reputation 0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; flag +union_ledger_public_

- **1956.01** `state_expire:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd

  _effects: state_remove bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:1024; flag -bureaucratic_ghost_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 30 -> 40; reputation -0.04000000000000001; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱)_

- **1956.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/pharmacy-gives-up` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig在柜台前站了很久，最后把处方留在那里，说等有货再来。回家路上经过医院，看见药房窗口也挂着“无货”；第二天，处方被雨水泡烂在口袋里。Hedwig把湿透的处方摊在炉边烤，字还是糊了。
  _effects: cash -1 (tram fare and a paper envelope for the prescription); health -0.04000000000000001 (another day without the prescribed medicine); condition; role; knowledge “医院药房也会挂“无货”；处方留在柜台，第二天下雨就泡烂了。”_

- **1956.08** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.bruised-apples` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  她蹲在菜市场湿漉漉的石板地上挑苹果，Elisabeth Winter 2从旁边经过，说这种碰伤的煮出来最甜。她回家把苹果切成块，锅里水刚开就下了糖精片，满屋子都是煮水果的酸香。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (买收摊前的碰伤苹果); food_

- **1957.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.04999999999999999 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019859_

  一年过去，Hedwig没有再点蜡烛，也没有再去那家后间。教堂的登记册翻到了新的一页，旧页边缘留着指甲划过的痕迹；街上的人开始讨论别的事。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 10 -> 2; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/back-to-work-too-soon` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig退烧第二天就回工厂，站到下午，眼前一阵阵发黑。回家后半夜又烧起来，她把湿毛巾搭在额头上，听见自己的心跳撞着枕头。第二天早上，Hedwig又去了，因为请假的扣款比药贵。
  _effects: cash 2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); health -0.1 (退烧后的第一碗粥); condition; flag +returned_to_work_too_soon_

  诊室在二楼，楼梯间飘着石炭酸味。医生把钳子举到灯下看了看，叫Hedwig攥紧扶手。Elisabeth Winter 2站在门边数墙上的价目表：拔牙两克朗五十赫勒，药棉五十赫勒，连消炎水另算。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.06 (拔除病牙解除疼痛)_

- **1957.09** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig把Elisabeth Winter 2和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Hedwig把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1957.11** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/speak-and-organize` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Hedwig在后屋的会议上报出自己街区的名字：谁家缺煤，谁被房东催租，谁知道哪间印刷厂还在招人。环城大道写字楼管理处把这些事记进一本旧账本，散会时每个人带走一叠更小的纸条。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.01999999999999999_

### 402 subject events

- **1915.06** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-sell-furniture-move` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  邻居用麻绳把锅和铺盖捆在一起，打了个死结。他说城南有更便宜的屋子，但离厂远。你蹲在地上把最后几件衣服塞进布袋，听见看门人在楼下把垃圾桶挪来挪去。
  _effects: cash 2.8 (卖旧家具所得); mood -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral_

- **1915.09** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/back-immediate-action` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef Huber把帽子捏在手里上台，说救济表格要填三张、盖章两处，煤却今晚就见底。年轻人拍椅子背，Emil Weiss在门口皱着眉。主持人记下赞成与反对的人数，纸板上的字越写越小。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.03_

- **1916.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef Huber和Gustav Huber在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Gustav Huber在门后放了一根木棍，Josef Huber把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood 0.04; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1916.01** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef Huber说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Berta Weiss没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef Huber听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1916.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Josef Huber靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费)_

- **1916.04** `r1-cul.confession::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/recognized-the-voice` tags: faith, confession, secrecy, shame, church, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Josef Huber说到一半，Rosa Lechner咳了一声，声音很熟。Josef Huber停下来问：是你吗。帘子后面沉默了很久，Rosa Lechner才说：在这里，我只是听的人。Josef Huber没有继续，站起来时把帘子带得晃了一下。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; condition; flag +recognized_priest_voice; relation rival with person-rosa-lechner; knowledge “the voice behind the curtain belongs to someone known outsid”_

- **1917.04** `fa17-cabaret.debut::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/small-part` tags: art, performance, cabaret, debut, reputation, failure, theatre, rural
  Hermann Kral只给了Josef Huber三句台词和一个端盘子的动作。排练时，Josef Huber把那三句念得太用力，导演让重来，说“你不是在演一个端盘子的人，你是在演一个怕被看见的人”。首演后没有人记住Josef Huber，但乐队指挥记住了Josef Huber每次上台前都在后台数拍子。
  _effects: state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:33; cash 1 (小角色的排练补贴); role; mood -0.020000000000000004; flag +debut_small_part; state_add fa17-muse state:world-vienna-1912:34; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1917.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef Huber看Anna Moser盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Anna Moser愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Josef Huber门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Josef Huber打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-anna-moser; food_

- **1917.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  护士把冰袋按在Josef Huber额上，水顺着鬓角流进衣领。Gustav Berg坐在床脚，数着窗外渐渐亮起的铺面门板，说回去给炉子添块煤。隔壁床的老头整夜在喊一个女人的名字。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.07999999999999999 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧)_

- **1917.11** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  汤锅冒着热气，Josef Huber盛出两勺倒进Gustav Berg带来的罐子里。Gustav Berg说了声谢谢就匆匆上楼。隔天罐子还回来，锅底搁着一小块猪油。
  _effects: food; mood 0.05_

- **1918.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Josef Huber把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Josef Huber读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1918.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/a-year-of-work` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  又一年过去，Josef Huber把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Josef Huber把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 42 -> 46; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 12 -> 15; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 55 -> 53; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000004_

  画室的门一关，时间就又变成Hermann Kral的。Josef Huber坐在同一把椅子上，听见窗外电车经过，数到第七次才被允许动一下；回家路上，Josef Huber发现自己的围巾上沾着一股松节油味，洗了两次也没掉。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 35 -> 41; state_parameter fa17-muse.inspiration 50 -> 52; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 10 -> 8; mood -0.03_

  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Josef Huber胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.030000000000000027 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1918.11** `vienna-illness-household.wage-earner-injury/walk-home` tags: health, family, home, injury
  没去医院，Josef Huber送Anton Winter回地下室，布条裹了伤指。妻子端来热水，Josef Huber却惦记着耽误的工时；临走时Anton Winter用另一只手碰了碰Josef Huber的胳膊。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; mood -0.03; health -0.030000000000000027 (伤口只经简单包扎); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1919.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef Huber和Gustav Huber在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Gustav Huber把煤钱推过来，Josef Huber接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood -0.03_

- **1919.01** `state_tick:fa17-muse/the-sitting-year/set-a-boundary` tags: fa17-muse, muse, art, desire, inspiration, dependence, vulnerability, beauty
  又一年过去，Josef Huber学会了在进画室前先问清楚这次要坐多久、画完给谁看。Hermann Kral嫌Josef Huber变得难安排，却也不得不把每次画像的时间写在纸上；那张纸后来被Josef Huber收进了自己的抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.autonomy 55 -> 60; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 8 -> 12; state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 41 -> 37; mood 0.03_

- **1919.03** `vienna-adult-social.wage-loan/wage-loan.refused` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Josef Huber在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的走廊里拦住Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne，话还没说完，对方就摆手说账上没这笔规矩。Josef Huber回车间时，工头正往布告栏上钉新的计件单。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation -0.05_

- **1919.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  炉子封小了，屋里凉得快，Josef Huber把大衣盖在被子上。安娜收碗时看见窗台上一排药瓶，没说什么，第二天多带了半块黑面包。楼下的管道工敲了一下午铁皮，咳嗽声混在里面听不真切。
  _effects: health 0.030000000000000027 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费); relation friend with person-maria-kral_

- **1919.07** `major-work-dismissal/short-time-instead` tags: work, dismissal, unemployment, major, employment
  厂里没有把Josef Huber的名字写进解雇名单，只把班次改成每周三天，工钱按日算，月底十八克朗。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说等订单回来再恢复；Josef Huber点头，把新的排班表折好，压在饭桌玻璃板下。Franz Lechner把自己的班次换给Josef Huber一天，下一天又换回去。房租没有按日算，面包也没有。
  _effects: employment Kurzarbeiter @ org-winter-print; role; cash -4 (lost days); condition; flag +short_time; relation colleague with person-franz-lechner_

- **1920.01** `major-work-injury/injury-compensation-refused` tags: work, injury, disability, unemployment, major
  事故簿上写着“擅自清理滚筒”。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Josef Huber先按手印，说按了才有医药费；Otto Berg 2说护罩早就坏了，保险会的职员没有记下。窗口里的职员翻了两页，把文件合上，说这属于重大过失。Josef Huber卖掉怀表付了十二克朗药费，右手从此伸不直，厂里也不再排班。
  _effects: health -0.18 (crushed right hand); condition; unemployment (dismissed after the accident report); cash -12 (medicine paid after the claim was refused); role; flag +compensation_denied; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1920.01** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef Huber和Gustav Huber谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Josef Huber家，钥匙只在白天给。Gustav Huber说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Friedrich Hofer听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Josef Huber把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.04999999999999999 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-helene-winter_

- **1920.08** `vienna-history-schnitzler-theatre/speak-in-the-foyer` tags: history, culture, arthur_schnitzler, burgtheater, theatre
  Josef Huber 在门廊里拦住Arthur Schnitzler，说第二幕的医生讲得太像真的。Arthur Schnitzler 停了一下，回答说他当过医生，知道那种语气；然后礼貌地告辞，没有留下地址。Josef Huber 看着他走进雨里，才想起自己忘了问下一部戏什么时候上演。
  _effects: knowledge “Schnitzler 当过医生，他的剧作里医生和官员的说话方式来自医院与部委的经验，而不是单纯的舞台效果。”; cash -3 (theatre ticket); reputation 0.01_

- **1921.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  浆糊是隔夜的，刷上去时带着股酸味。Josef Huber把纸四角按平，有人凑近念出“一九〇一年，欠三十四克朗”。办公室里的椅子腿不平，坐在上面的人一动就晃，可没人起身。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; flag +union_ledger_public_

- **1921.02** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef Huber说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Friedrich Hofer没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef Huber听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1921.09** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Josef Huber把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Friedrich Weiss替Josef Huber扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1921.12** `p9-history-medical-daily.house-call/carry-child-hospital` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily, injury
  Josef Huber用毯子裹住Josef Stein，搀着往综合医院急诊走，走到半路干脆背起来。长椅上挤满人，等到窗户发白，Josef Stein的烧退了些，Josef Huber的胳膊却抖得端不住水杯；雪灌进后领，鞋底磨得只剩一层布，这一夜把两个人的人情结结实实绑在了一起。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (雪夜背孩子去医院，肩臂脱力); mood 0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

  又一年过去，Josef Huber把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Josef Huber把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。

- **1922.06** `vienna-illness-household.wage-earner-injury/walk-home` tags: health, family, home
  Josef Huber把Emil Kral扶回地下室，用干净布条裹住伤指。家里人连声道谢，Josef Huber心里却盘算明天怎么跟Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说；布条很快洇出一点红。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; mood -0.029999999999999995; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1922.06** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Alois Lechner把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Josef Huber把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Alois Lechner低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); flag +rent_increase_accepted; health -0.020000000000000018 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase)_

- **1922.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/helps_fetch_doctor` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  隔壁Karl Kraus的小儿子烧到说胡话，额头烫得能烘手。Karl Kraus在楼道里抹眼泪，说请不起医生。Josef Huber下楼叫了街角药房的伙计，花四十赫勒买了一包退烧药粉，又帮着用湿布敷了一整夜。天亮时孩子退了烧，Karl Kraus端来一碗热汤面，面底下卧着一个鸡蛋。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买退烧药粉); health -0.020000000000000018 (熬夜照料病人); relation friend with person-karl-kraus_

- **1922.11** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef Huber把通知念给Rosa Lechner听，Rosa Lechner摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Josef Huber去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.03_

- **1923.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Ferdinand Hofer把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Josef Huber打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (高烧硬熬伤身); condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1923.01** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Friedrich Hofer敲了门。Josef Huber掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Friedrich Hofer什么也没说就走了。夜里Josef Huber听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; mood -0.05_

  Josef Huber在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Josef Huber去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role_

- **1923.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef Huber刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Maria Kral一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Maria Kral的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-maria-kral; mood -0.05_

- **1924.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef Huber把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Maria Kral抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Josef Huber把床单对折，腾出一半，Maria Kral用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; mood 0.020000000000000004_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:001724_

  第一趟过街口时车上的煤箱差点翻下来，Hermann Kral用膝盖顶住，Josef Huber把绳子又收了一道。第三趟回来，两人靠在门框上喘气，鞋底都是灰，省下的钱还揣在围裙里。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); health -0.050000000000000044 (三趟推车扛家具累得肩臂酸痛); relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral_

- **1924.05** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.salt` tags: home, market, food, social
  腌鲱鱼切成两段，一段今天吃，一段留着。黑面包硬得要用刀锯。Berta Huber来借盐，眼睛落在鱼上，说可以用半棵卷心菜换一块。Josef Huber切了一指宽递过去。
  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food_

- **1924.06** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Friedrich Stein在午休时把本子摊在Josef Huber的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Josef Huber从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Friedrich Stein当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; reputation 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1924.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  衙门走廊的地砖是黑白相间的，Josef Huber站着站着就把重心从一只脚换到另一只脚。窗口里的办事员姓什么没人知道，只叫“三号窗”。表递进去时，里面说“等复核”，声音很平。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在衙门走廊站了大半天); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1925.01** `sm18-object.tram-lost::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/advertises-in-the-paper` tags: object, loss, child, tram, family, everyday, reform, medical
  Josef Huber在《新维也纳日报》的失物栏里登了三行小字，花了四十个铜板。第二天，一个卖旧货的人说在电车终点站见过那样的铜牌。Mitzi Hofer放学后跟着Josef Huber去找，一路上把书包带绕在手指上，绕得发白。
  _effects: cash -1 (在报纸上登失物启事); state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:70; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000299:child; mood -0.01999999999999999; flag +advertised_for_the_childs_medal; flag +something_is_missing_

  Josef Huber把Otto Berg的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Otto Berg今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Josef Huber听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 59 -> 67; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 38 -> 35; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1925.03** `ml18-luck.wrong-horse::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-wrong-horse-wins` tags: gambling, luck, money, horse_race, absurd, windfall, reform, medical
  Josef Huber把“蓝多瑙”看成了“蓝星期一”，在柜台前把五克朗押在一匹没人看好的马上。最后一道弯，那匹马从外道切进来，Friedrich Hofer手里的铅笔停在票面上。赔率是一赔十二；Gustav Huber把赢来的钱数了两遍，Josef Huber还在看自己的票，像不相信上面的名字。
  _effects: cash 60 (winnings from the wrong horse at long odds); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:71; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; reputation 0.03; flag +wrong_horse_won; mood 0.09000000000000002; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.03_

- **1925.04** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef Huber在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Karl Kraus却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Josef Huber把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1925.04** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 42 -> 45; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  街区的查执照的人来查无线电执照。Josef Huber干脆请他坐下，把音量调小，正好播到天气预报。对方听完一段，说下次记得去登记，没有开罚单；Anna Moser在门口撇嘴，说这机器以后归大家听。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004; flag +radio_official_visit_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/a-year-of-work` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  又一年过去，Josef Huber把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Josef Huber把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 58 -> 62; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 24 -> 27; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 47 -> 45; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/a-receipt-in-the-lining` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  半年过去，Josef Huber又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 25 -> 31; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 5 -> 3; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1926.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef Huber把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Luise Lechner借给Josef Huber一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Josef Huber睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); mood -0.06_

- **1926.06** `r2-rural.communal-oven::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/baked-at-the-communal-oven` tags: rural, bread, community, scarcity, neighbor, agriculture, small_town, peace
  Ferdinand Hofer把长木铲伸进炉膛，面包在石板上鼓起来。Josef Huber交了一铜板，拿到一只烫手的面包，用围裙兜着往家走。Friedrich Hofer排在后面，问能不能借一把火，Ferdinand Hofer说火不借，面包可以赊。
  _effects: cash -1 (公共烤炉的排队); food; flag +baked_at_communal_oven; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1926.10** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef Huber和Maria Huber谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Josef Huber家，钥匙只在白天给。Maria Huber说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Friedrich Hofer听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Josef Huber把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1926.11** `bc18-inherit.forty-seven-franz::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/bury-the-stranger` tags: inheritance, mistaken_identity, debt, funeral, work, absurd, family, rural
  遗嘱里还附了一笔丧葬费，条件是要有“家属”到场。死者没有亲人，Josef Huber只好穿黑衣站在墓旁，听牧师念一个从未听过的名字。雨把花圈上的纸花打湿，泥水漫过鞋面；葬礼结束后，Franz Lechner 2把九克朗交给他，说账已结清。Josef Huber回家脱鞋，发现袜子上沾着别人的土。
  _effects: cash 7 (以家属身份到场后的遗产余款); reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; condition; flag +buried_a_stranger_for_the_estate; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  半年过去，Josef Huber又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 31 -> 37; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 3 -> 1; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1927.01** `p9-history-public-action.organize-relief/church-hall` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  两赫勒一碗，赊账用铅笔记在账本边上。Josef Huber 把账本挂在门钉上，风吹得纸页翻动，Anna Moser 用围裙擦手，招呼排队的老人先坐。
  _effects: cash -4 (垫付首日面粉和柴火); food; reputation 0.07; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser_

- **1927.07** `oj18-taxi.night-fare::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/coop-keys` tags: taxi, night, driver, passenger, work, odd_job, everyday, rural
  合作社的调度Elisabeth Weiss 2把一枚铜牌和一把车钥匙推过柜台，说夜班只跑环城路，十一点以后不接去普拉特的车。Josef Huber把铜牌挂在后视镜上，第一晚就拉了七个客人，最后一个在车上睡着，打鼾像一台小马达。
  _effects: organization_found 第二区夜班出租合作社 (other); position 夜班司机 @taxi:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2-evt:world-vienna-1912:002784; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:122; cash 2 (night taxi fares); flag +taxi_night_shift; knowledge “夜班只跑环城路，十一点以后不接去普拉特的车。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1927.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  Josef Huber说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Friedrich Hofer一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: health -0.03999999999999998 (高烧硬熬伤身); relation friend with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1927.09** `hs18-queue.figure::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/give-your-place` tags: historical_figure, queue, recognition, bread, witness, history, rural, agriculture
  队伍往前挪时，Josef Huber认出前面那个把帽檐压低的人——报纸上见过那张脸，Sigmund Freud。对方手里也攥着一张配给证，指节冻得发白。Josef Huber把自己的位置让出来，说自己去另一条队；Sigmund Freud点了一下头，没有报名字。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:123; relation acquaintance with person-sigmund-freud; flag +yielded_place_to_historical_figure_

- **1927.12** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Anna Weiss怪Josef Huber把人都招到楼梯上，木头泡了潮气才裂的。Josef Huber没还嘴，蹲下去看那道裂缝，指甲刮下一层朽木屑。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; mood -0.06_

- **1928.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-vote-in-the-corridor` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, rural, agriculture
  Gustav Moser 2在走廊里凑够了票数，把Josef Huber负责的那间办公室转给了一个更听话的人。会议记录写得很干净：因工作需要。Josef Huber回到办公室时，门牌已经换了，钥匙还能插进去，只是转不动。
  _effects: reputation -0.07999999999999999; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; condition; flag +office_quietly_taken; cash -1 (重新置办办公用品的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  一年过去，Josef Huber已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Josef Huber也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 35; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 15; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 12; cash 1 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Josef Huber又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 80 -> 82_

- **1928.01** `state_expire:fa17-starving-artist` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition

- **1928.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Josef Huber把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Josef Huber忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 1 -> 9; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 37 -> 35; mood -0.02999999999999997_

- **1928.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Josef Huber走到Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1928.03** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef Huber数出八赫勒交给Josefine Hofer，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Josefine Hofer说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Josef Huber盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); flag +funeral_society_member_

- **1928.11** `p9-history-union-political.reform-meeting/reform-read-petition` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  提问时间一到，Josef Huber站起来，把请愿书上的三条逐条读出：房租上限、成年男子普选、十小时工时。Friedrich Lechner 2在台上让记录员一条条记下，礼堂里有人跟着低声重复那三个数目。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; knowledge “市政厅旁的会议上，官员让记录员把三条请愿要求记进了正式记录”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1928.12** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Anna Bauer敲了门。Josef Huber掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Anna Bauer什么也没说就走了。夜里Josef Huber听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1929.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/clean-audit` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, reform, medical
  Friedrich Lechner 2带了两只空箱子来，走的时候还是空的。Josef Huber把三年的合同、收据和会议记录按月份排在地上，Friedrich Lechner 2抽查了十二份，每一份都能对上签字人；最后一句是“下次可以少准备一点”。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; organization_funds; skill; mood 0.03; flag +passed_audit; knowledge “审计抽查了十二份合同，每一份都能对上签字人。”_

- **1929.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Josef Huber忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Josef Huber听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 82 -> 74; mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1929.01** `state_expire:sm18-missing-object` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday

- **1929.01** `r13-civ.quarantine::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/broke-quarantine` tags: health, quarantine, epidemic, neighbor, confinement, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef Huber等到夜里，从后院的柴门溜出去上工。车间里没有人问，机器照常转，只有换班的人往旁边让了半步。回家时白线还在，Josef Huber跨过去，鞋底带回一层湿泥。
  _effects: cash 3 (门框上的石灰线); reputation -0.1; flag +broke_quarantine; knowledge “the workshop does not ask why a quarantined worker returned”_

- **1929.09** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef Huber和Josef Weiss谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Josef Huber家，钥匙只在白天给。Josef Weiss说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Alois Huber听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Josef Huber把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1929.09** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/stay-for-the-rent` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  东西还到一半，两人算了算房租、煤钱和两个人的工钱凑在一起才够。最后钥匙没收回去，外套也留下；这个月能少花一克朗，话说到“以后”两个字就断了，谁都不往下接，炉子今晚还是得两个人一起生。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; cash 1 (合租省下一份房租)_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Josef Huber忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Josef Huber听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 74 -> 66; mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1930.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Josef Huber去了Berggasse 19, Wohnung und Ordination，坐在后排听完了关于弗洛伊德周围的医生、作家和学生以周三讨论会、病例和出版物形成一个小型知识网络的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:seed-vienna-psychoanalytic-circle-1912 as participant; knowledge “我参加了弗洛伊德周围的医生、作家和学生以周三讨论会、病例和出版物形成一个小型知识网络。。”; mood 0.03_

- **1930.05** `p9-history-medical-daily.house-call/wait-with-wet-cloth` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Josef Huber用湿布和半瓶退烧药熬到天亮，炉子多烧了一筐煤，锅里的粥也多添了两把面，口粮下去一截。Anna Huber 2来时只在门口站了站，留一张饮食单，说继续观察，出诊费免了。Josef Huber整夜没合眼，Otto Berg 2的烧慢慢退下去；桌上多了一张纸，炉边少了一筐煤。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1931.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signed-with-margin` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, reform, medical
  Josef Huber在预算表上签了字，把冬季煤房的款项从三笔并成一笔，又给夜班守卫加了半份津贴。Franz Lechner 4算出省下的钱刚好够修屋顶；没有人鼓掌，但一月的会议室不再需要穿大衣。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill; mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +signed_balanced_budget; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1931.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef Huber在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Emil从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 66 -> 68_

- **1931.03** `ml18-investment.transylvanian-mine::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-mine-strikes-gold` tags: investment, mining, fraud, money, risk, business, reform, medical
  Ferdinand Hofer把一小截岩芯放在桌上，说特兰西瓦尼亚的矿脉比图纸上厚三倍，只是缺一台抽水机。Franz Lechner 2验过矿样，Josef Huber把积蓄的一半投进去。八个月后，第一批金砂运到维也纳，Josef Huber在交易所门口看见自己的名字被写在行情板上，后面跟着一个从未见过的数字。
  _effects: cash 90 (the first gold shipment from the mine); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:238; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; reputation 0.05000000000000002; flag +mine_struck_gold; mood 0.09999999999999998; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.02999999999999997_

  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Berta Weiss排在前面，回头替Josef Huber看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Josef Huber用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1932.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil嫌Josef Huber的床单滴水滴到Emil家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Josef Huber下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Josef Huber把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Emil那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004401_

  人事处翻出一张一九〇七年的任命书：助理文书的位子一直没撤，上一任在退休后仍每月来领一次钥匙。Josef Huber把Josef的名字补在下面，第二天，两个人一起打开了那间堆满未拆信件的办公室。
  _effects: position 助理文书 @taxi:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2-evt:world-vienna-1912:002784; flag +inherited_ghost_post; knowledge “助理文书的职位从一九〇七年起就没有正式撤销，柜子里还有未拆的公务信件。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; flag +permit_for_favor; knowledge “许可证少了一页排水图，盖章时没有补。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  旧货摊收了一半，铁皮盒里的零钱叮当作响。Josef Huber问那只描金边的杯子，摊主用手背往街角一指，说刚有个女人挑走了，还说那裂纹其实拿胶补补能用。她低头看摊布上留下的一个圆印，是杯子搁久了压出来的。

  Mitzi Huber在Berggasse 19, Wohnung und Ordination生下一个孩子，取名Elisabeth Huber。Josef Huber在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Josef Huber从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。

  _effects: position_end 夜班司机 @taxi:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2-evt:world-vienna-1912:002784; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood -0.04000000000000001; condition; flag +left_office_in_order; knowledge “移交清单上最后一项是印章，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把。”_

  Josef Huber把十二只木箱码在板车上，用麻绳捆了两道，轮子碾过石子路时，箱角互相磕着响。走到第三道街口，车轴响了一声，Elisabeth Weiss 2蹲下去紧了紧销子。货送到时比约定早半个钟头，店主验完封条，在送货单上画了押。

  _effects: cash -3 (凑钱买了一双结实的工靴); health 0.019999999999999997 (脚上有了不漏水的靴子)_

  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Josef Huber把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Mitzi Huber站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Josef Huber手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。

  捐款的事刚说出口，家里的兄弟先来了。他说这笔钱该留给自家人，不该拿去给街上的孩子看病。Friedrich Hofer在诊所门口等了半小时，最后把图纸卷起来；Josef Huber站在空铺面里，听见隔壁肉铺的锯骨声一下一下传过来。

  _effects: cash -4 (替友人偿还部分赌债（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000078:child; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1935.03** `bc18-enemy.accidental-help::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/letter-printed-as-praise` tags: enemy, mistake, work, reputation, absurd, luck, social, vienna
  Friedrich Weiss写了一封告状信，列数Josef Huber的懒惰、贪婪和“可疑的出身”，却把信封上的地址写成了报社。Friedrich Hofer以为这是一篇自荐小品，删掉几处脏话登了出来；读者喜欢这种不留情面的自嘲，两个商行给Josef Huber寄来聘书。Friedrich Weiss在咖啡馆看见报纸，差点把杯子捏碎。
  _effects: cash 4 (商行聘书的预付薪金); reputation 0.04999999999999999; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:363; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; condition; flag +enemys_letter_became_an_advert; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash 12 (养满两年鹦鹉得到的遗产); skill; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; condition; flag +kept_the_parrot; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

  纸牌桌上连开三把庄。Josef Huber按自己写在烟盒背面的翻倍法，把最后十五克朗押在闲上。庄家翻开一张四，闲家九点；Mitzi Huber的手在桌下抓住Josef Huber的袖子；Josef Huber把赢回的三十克朗换成现钱，数出十克朗塞进袜筒，剩下的放进内袋，说今晚到此为止。
  _effects: cash 30 (winnings taken off the card table); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:362; reputation 0.020000000000000018; flag +left_the_table_ahead; mood 0.07999999999999999; flag -sudden_fortune; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.03_

- **1936.01** `bc18-fame.lecture-tour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/provincial-triumph` tags: fame, lecture, travel, absurd, public, invention, reputation, vienna
  外省的市民协会一个接一个发来邀请，请Josef Huber讲“发明的未来”。Franz Lechner 3把票价定得太高，礼堂却照样坐满；Josef Huber在台上谈摩擦、谈火花、谈一种自己也没见过的机器，听众把每一句都记进小本子。散场后，Friedrich Hofer握着手说，这座城市需要更多像Josef Huber这样敢想的人。

- **1936.01** `state_reaction:bc18-ridiculous-fame/reprinted-wrong` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  报纸的周末副刊又把Josef Huber的旧照片翻出来，配上一个更整齐的故事：发明、失败、谦逊的晚年。Josef Huber写信去更正，编辑回了一张便条，说读者喜欢现在这个版本。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 28 -> 34; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.absurdity 70 -> 75; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 10 -> 6; flag +story_reprinted_wrong; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Josef Huber仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Josef Huber学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 34 -> 41; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 3 -> 4; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000009; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `r13-fam.look-for-lost-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/name-on-list` tags: task, family, missing, official, war, search, vienna, urban
  Josef Huber替Mitzi Lechner 3把名字、出生年和最后住址写在纸上，递进窗口。Friedrich Hofer翻了三本名册，手指停在一行被水洇开的字上，说这个人登记在临时收容所。Mitzi Lechner 3听见时，手里的伞掉在地上；Josef Huber替她把伞捡起来，才发现自己的手也在抖。
  _effects: cash -2 (念错的名字); flag +found_missing_relative_on_list; role; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; knowledge “the missing relative was registered at a transit shelter”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1936.04** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef Huber只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Otto Winter手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Otto Winter把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Josef Huber记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; flag +seeking_work_

- **1936.07** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/child-at-the-bear-pit` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  一个男孩翻过熊池的矮栏去捡帽子。Josef Huber没有喊，怕惊动母熊，只提着装苹果的桶绕到下风处，把桶敲得咚咚响。母熊转身时，孩子被Alois Huber一把拽了上来；Josef Huber的腿在后来的很多年里还会在梦里发软。
  _effects: health -0.04000000000000001 (the bear-pit rescue left the legs shaking); reputation 0.06; cash 2 (reward from the child's parents); state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:388; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +zoo_night_shift; flag +saved_child_at_zoo; knowledge “母熊被苹果桶引开时，孩子已经挂在{keeper}的胳膊上了。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1936.07** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.sheet-quarrel` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef Huber发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Josef Kraus问是谁动的。Josef Kraus说是Elisabeth Bauer嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000415:child_

- **1937.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Josef Huber胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Josef Huber的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Josef Huber想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 38 -> 30; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 65 -> 60; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +odd_job_scare_

  厂门口的砖墙被太阳晒得发烫。Anton在旁听着，插了一句这后生看着手稳。工头把烟灰弹进阴沟，说试半天，午饭他管。Josef Huber点头时，肚子先响了一声。
  _effects: flag +trial_work_pending; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1937.06** `bc18-crime.failed-burglary::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/right-office-wrong-drawer` tags: crime, burglary, mistake, absurd, police, legal, comic, vienna
  他们终于摸到公证处的后窗，撬开的抽屉里却没有遗嘱，只有一叠慈善捐款收据，数目和报纸上公布的对不上。Josef Huber把收据塞进外套，找到一位律师，换回七克朗；律师说，这不是盗窃，是取证。Anton问那遗嘱怎么办，Josef Huber说，先让死人再等一等。
  _effects: cash 7 (把慈善收据交给律师的酬劳); knowledge “公证处抽屉里的慈善捐款收据与报纸公布的数目对不上。”; relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; condition; flag +stole_charity_receipts; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1937.07** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.lost-dog/lost-dog.chase-two-streets` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, animal, neighbor, injury
  狗跑得不算快，可它拐弯时总挑窄巷子钻。Josef Huber跟着它穿过一条挂满洗好的床单的过道，床单扫过她的额头。等她站定，狗已经不见影了，脚上的旧鞋咧开了嘴，露出沾了泥的袜尖。
  _effects: health -0.01999999999999999 (追狗跑得脚底发疼); cash -0.6 (补鞋底的开销); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

  火车在清晨进站，Friedrich Hofer把Josef Huber的护照翻到最后一页，又看了看那张没有照片的出生纸。蓝章落下去，手续费和住址登记表一起被推到窗口；Josef Huber把临时居留纸折成四折，塞进内衣口袋，走出站门时天还没有完全亮。

- **1938.01** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Helene Bauer在午休时把本子摊在Josef Huber的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Josef Huber从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Helene Bauer当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000724:child; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

  _effects: flag +has_work_lead; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

  一年过去，Josef Huber已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Josef Huber也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。

  一年过去，Josef Huber能在菜市场用德语讲价，也知道哪家铺子肯赊半块黄油。房东仍叫他“新来的”，但楼下的面包师已经记住了他买哪一种面包，还会把前一天剩的那只小圆面包留出来。

  Emil Stein在门房里听完，从抽屉里摸出一张印着厂名的信纸，写Josef Huber“手脚干净，肯学机器”，又签了名。墨水没干，他用吸墨纸按了按，说别拿去给太大的厂，小厂才看这个。Josef Huber把信折成四折，放进内袋最里面。

  夜班结束前，Josef Huber听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。

  衙门走廊的地砖是黑白相间的，Josef Huber站着站着就把重心从一只脚换到另一只脚。窗口里的办事员姓什么没人知道，只叫“三号窗”。表递进去时，里面说“等复核”，声音很平。
  _effects: health -0.02999999999999997 (在衙门走廊站了大半天); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1939.01** `major-family.child-death/child-death.scalded` tags: family, child_death, grief, major, lifecycle, death, injury
  洗衣日，灶上的大锅刚烧开。Katharina Huber在门槛上绊了一跤，整锅水从肩上浇下来。Josef Huber用手去撕衣服，连自己的掌心也烫掉了皮。孩子熬到半夜，两人都怪对方没看好孩子，Elisabeth Weiss 3蹲在楼梯口，谁也没有先开口；第二天，楼里的洗衣妇把锅刷了三遍。
  _effects: death (scalded by boiling laundry water); health -0.04999999999999999 (burned hands); condition; mood -0.2; role; cash -7 (doctor and burial)_

- **1939.01** `oj18-zoo.escape::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/chimp-on-the-prater` tags: zoo, animals, escape, night, blame, work, odd_job, vienna
  黑猩猩“卡斯帕”用一根弯钉挑开第二道门，顺着排水沟跑进普拉特。Josef Huber提着一桶苹果追到旋转木马后面，看见它坐在售票亭顶上，正把一张张票根往嘴里塞。Maria Berg赶来时，Josef Huber已经用苹果把它引进了送货马车。
  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 32 -> 40; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 31 -> 36; cash -1 (apples used to lure the chimpanzee); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; flag +monkey_returned; knowledge “卡斯帕会用弯钉挑门闩，苹果比网子管用。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Josef Huber把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

  Josef Huber在楼里替教父Otto Berg问了一圈，最后在洗衣妇家找到一张空床，每月三克朗。Otto Berg把箱子搬过去，Josef Huber替他垫了第一个月的房租；两人在楼梯口分开，Otto Berg说等找到工就还。床板短了一截，箱子只能竖着放。

  胶片在片门里起火，火苗顺着观察窗舔进大厅。Josef Huber没有先去拿灭火沙，而是把后排的孩子往侧门推；Luise Hofer用湿毯子捂住片盒，手背烧掉一层皮。第二天，Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens把烧黑的片盒留在柜台上，说“留着，提醒我们”。

  Josef Huber去了Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus，坐在后排听完了关于Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreich的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1933 as participant; knowledge “我参加了Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SD。”; mood 0.03_

- **1940.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef Huber把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Emil Kral 2借给Josef Huber一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Josef Huber睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); mood -0.06_

- **1940.05** `p9-history-medical-daily.house-call/wait-with-wet-cloth` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily, injury
  Josef Huber用湿布和半瓶退烧药熬到天亮，炉子多烧了一筐煤，锅里的粥也多添了两把面，口粮下去一截。Anton Stein来时只在门口站了站，留一张饮食单，说继续观察，出诊费免了。Josef Huber整夜没合眼，Hermann Huber的烧慢慢退下去；桌上多了一张纸，炉边少了一筐煤。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (整夜守候，睡眠不足); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006377:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1940.07** `r14-soc.courtyard-rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/slapped-in-the-courtyard` tags: rumor, reputation, neighbor_conflict, shame, gender, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef Huber在院子里拦住Rosa Berg，让对方把话再说一遍。Rosa Berg笑着重复，Josef Huber的手先落下去。晾衣绳上的床单被扯下来，盖住了一只煤桶。Friedrich Kral尖叫着去叫看门人，孩子们趴在每一扇窗后面看。看门人赶来时，Rosa Berg已经退到煤堆后面，脸上留着一道红印。看门人把两人分开，谁也没有去捡那条床单。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; reputation -0.07999999999999996; condition; flag +slapped_rumor_monger; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

- **1940.08** `oj18-mortuary.first-body::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/municipal-mortuary` tags: death, mortuary, funeral, work, odd_job, night, everyday, vienna
  市立太平间缺一个夜班杂役，Luise Hofer 2只问Josef Huber怕不怕冷。地下室的抽屉一排十二个，有的拉开了就不再推回去；Josef Huber的工作是登记遗物、给无名尸挂上编号，再在天亮前把地拖干净。
  _effects: organization_found 市立太平间 (state); position 太平间杂役 @morgue:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2-evt:world-vienna-1912:009110; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:534; cash 2 (municipal night wages); condition; flag +mortuary_worker; knowledge “太平间地下室的抽屉一排十二个，无名尸也要有编号。”; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1941.01** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-and-mother-weak` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  孩子出生后取名Anton Huber，Elisabeth Weiss 3一直出血。Josef Huber跑去医院，医生来晚了，只留下药和十八克朗的账单；Elisabeth Weiss 3落下产后虚弱的毛病，Josef Huber抱着孩子坐在床边，直到邻居把汤送来，才发现自己的鞋穿反了。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.2 (postpartum bleeding); cash -18 (doctor and medicine); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:575_

- **1941.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Josef Huber听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 30 -> 38; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 20 -> 17; mood -0.03; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009698_

- **1941.01** `office16-appointment.patronage-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/list-rejected` tags: office, appointment, patronage, organization, politics, power, vienna, urban
  名单在委员会卡住了。Maria Huber 2的名字被划掉，理由是“没有相关资历”；Josef Huber没有争，只把名单收回公文包。晚上，Maria Huber 2来问结果，Josef Huber说再等一年；Maria Huber 2没有坐，站了一会儿就走了。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.03; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; flag +appointment_list_rejected; knowledge “委员会以“没有相关资历”为由划掉了联络员的名字。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1941.01** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Otto Berg在午休时把本子摊在Josef Huber的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Josef Huber从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Otto Berg当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000134:child; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Josef Huber的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Josef Huber想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。

  签名纸在电车上被挤掉一角，最后十个名字不见了。他站在终点站找了一刻钟，只在座位下找到一张被踩脏的纸；Gustav Huber 2说“算了”，他还是把剩下的名字重新抄了一遍。他把重抄的名单交给Gustav Huber 2，让对方再核一遍。
  _effects: flag +petition_signatures_lost; reputation -0.010000000000000009; cash -0.5 (重抄签名纸和电车票); health -0.010000000000000009 (在终点站找纸); knowledge “签名纸要夹在硬纸板里，电车挤掉的一角找不回来”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

- **1941.06** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Anna Stein凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Josef Huber把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

- **1941.06** `statistical_infection`
  Josef Huber在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1941.09** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef Huber从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  长桌摆在同乡会租来的后间，桌上有一锅卷心菜炖肉和两种面包。Elisabeth Bauer收下Josef Huber的会费，把名字写进一本线装簿子，又在旁边记下“会德语，能跑邮局”；散会时，有人把一位律师的地址塞给他。

  Josef Huber把咳嗽归罪于煤灰，把腿肿归罪于站得太久。Elisabeth Bauer说最好少上半天工，Josef Huber笑了一声，说“半天工的钱谁给”。第二天照旧出门，口袋里那张处方被钥匙磨出一道白边。

  Berta Huber烧了两天，喉咙里长出灰白色的膜。Josef Huber去药房买血清，药剂师说要医院处方。等Josef Huber排到门诊，孩子已经喘不上气。医生用小勺压住舌头看了一眼，摇了摇头。回家路上，Josef Huber把没拆封的药包一直攥在手里。
  _effects: cash -6 (小棺材抬过院子); health -0.040000000000000036 (小棺材抬过院子); condition; flag +child_died_of_diphtheria; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; death (白喉窒息)_

  Josef Huber在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Josef Kral拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失)_

  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); skill_

  夜里队伍被赶到路沟里，给一列军火车让道。Josef Weiss蹲下去就起不来，Josef Huber把她的手臂架在肩上，走了两百步，两个人都倒在雪里。Luise Huber用手电照了照，说“别停，停下就冻住了”。Josef Huber把Josef Weiss的鞋带系在自己手腕上，拖着走。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (公路边的开水锅); flag +carried_elder_on_night_march; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; knowledge “the column must keep moving when trains pass”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

  无名死者在前一天下葬，今天却有一封律师信追到殡仪馆：死者把一只铁盒留给“替我合上眼睛的人”。Luise Hofer 2打开盒子，里面是六克朗、三张从未寄出的明信片和一枚女式胸针；Josef Huber只拿了明信片，把钱留给公墓买一块便宜的石牌。

- **1945.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Josef Huber听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 18; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 22 -> 19; mood -0.03; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_

- **1945.03** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/signed-for-a-favor` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Otto Lechner在签字前把一张剧院包厢的票根留在桌上，说是“给孩子的”。Josef Huber把票根推回去，却在许可证上签了名；排水图仍缺一页，但窗口当天就盖了章。扩建开工后，第一场雨把后院淹了半尺。
  _effects: organization_funds; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; knowledge “许可证少了一页排水图，盖章时没有补。”; mood 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1945.04** `bc18-crime.inept-blackmail::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pay-hush-money` tags: crime, blackmail, photograph, mistake, humiliation, absurd, reputation, vienna
  Josef Huber先给了两克朗，Friedrich Kral又要四克朗；第三次来收钱时，Josef Huber把他带到了警察局。警员在Friedrich Kral的口袋里找到三张别人的照片，其中一张是市议员在酒馆后门。Josef Huber拿回自己的照片，也花掉了一个下午，回家时晚饭已经凉了。
  _effects: cash -4 (付给勒索者的封口钱); reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; condition; flag +paid_then_reported_blackmail; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1945.11** `oj18-mortuary.first-body::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/coffin-coop` tags: death, mortuary, funeral, work, odd_job, night, everyday, vienna
  师傅Emil Stein把一块冷毛巾塞进Josef Huber手里，让Josef Huber先擦死者的脸，再擦自己的。殡葬合作社的规矩写在门背后：先入殓，后结账；无名者也要有一块写着日期的木牌。Josef Huber做完第一具，手抖得点不着烟。
  _effects: organization_found 圣马克斯殡葬合作社 (other); position 入殓工 @mortuary:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2-evt:world-vienna-1912:012206; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:733; cash 1 (first mortuary wages); skill; knowledge “殡葬合作社的规矩：先入殓，后结账；无名者也要有一块日期木牌。”; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1946.01** `oj18-porter.files::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/returned-briefcase` tags: hospital, porter, papers, police, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  Hedwig出院时把一只公文包落在候诊椅上。Josef Huber追到门口，Hedwig接过包，先看拉链，再看Josef Huber的脸，然后从钱包里抽出一张钞票。Josef Huber收下了，因为那张钞票正好够买一个月的止咳糖浆。
  _effects: cash 2 (a reward for returning the briefcase); state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 25 -> 30; flag +returned_briefcase; knowledge “公文包的主人先检查拉链，再检查我的脸，然后才给钱。”_

  显影液被Anna Stein碰翻，整盆药水泼在一张婚礼合影上。新郎的脸变成一团灰雾，店主抓住Josef Huber的领子；Elisabeth Bauer来后，两人被关了一夜。第二天，Josef Huber交了罚款三克朗，Anna Stein被新娘的家人追着要赔照片；那顶帽子也留在了拘留室。

- **1946.07** `r2-law.eviction-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::r2-law.eviction-hearing-reform/rent_control_defense` tags: law, eviction, housing, court, tenement, vienna, urban, postwar
  新设的租金法庭只看两件事：租金是否超过管制线，房屋是否适合居住。Luise Huber让Elisabeth Winter 2出示登记卡，Elisabeth Winter 2拿不出来；Josef Huber把登记处的回执递上去，租金正好超了三成。Luise Huber判多收部分抵扣欠租，Elisabeth Winter 2当场说要上诉。
  _effects: cash 4 (驱逐听证的十五分钟); flag +used_rent_control_defense_in_eviction; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; knowledge “unregistered rent above the control line can be offset again”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118_

- **1946.10** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef Huber看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Grete Winter追出来问怎么不领了，Josef Huber说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Josef Huber把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; food_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013107_

- **1947.01** `df18-luck.borrowed-for-a-child::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/keep-the-loan` tags: luck, debt, family, child, illness, poverty, care, vienna
  Josef Huber还没去借钱，Anton Huber的烧自己退了。Emil Moser还是把钱塞过来，说留着买营养品。Josef Huber把钱收进糖罐，没舍得花；月底房租不够时，才从里面抽出三克朗。那张烟盒纸一直压在罐底，像一个没有说出口的借条。
  _effects: cash 3 (邻居塞来的钱，月底抽出三克朗交房租); state_add df18-borrowed-luck state:world-vienna-1912:799; health 0.020000000000000018 (孩子自己退了烧); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; flag +df18_kept_loan_for_rent; mood 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; flag +df18_luck_borrowed; knowledge “我得到了一点不该白拿的好运，代价还没有说清。”_

- **1947.01** `df18-blessing.laundry-water::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/repaint-the-wall` tags: blessing, omen, water, faith, fraud, consolation, religious, eerie
  本堂神父来看过，说只是墙里的水管渗水，叫人把墙重新刷了。水渍被白灰盖住，洗衣房亮了一截。Josef Huber站在梯子下面，觉得少了点什么，又觉得这样也好。那天晚上，Josef Huber睡得很早，没有点蜡烛。
  _effects: health 0.010000000000000009 (洗衣房不再潮湿); mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “水渍只是渗水，神父让人把墙刷了。”; flag +df18_water_stain_repainted; condition_

  _effects: health -0.06 (chronic illness worsened); cash -2 (medical costs); state_parameter chronic-illness.severity 0.5 -> 0.6_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Josef Huber的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Josef Huber想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 22 -> 14; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 50 -> 45; mood -0.03_

- **1947.04** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef Huber只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Alois Weiss手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Alois Weiss把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Josef Huber记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food_

- **1947.04** `state_reaction:oj18-workplace-legend/the-story-turns` tags: oj18-workplace-legend, work, legend, odd_job, rumor, identity, night, state
  酒馆里有人把另一件旧事也讲成了Josef Huber的遭遇：同样的雨夜、同样的钥匙，连结尾都差不多。Josef Huber听见时没有打断，只把杯子往桌角推了推。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.legend 33 -> 38; state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.truth 65 -> 62; mood -0.01999999999999999; knowledge “有人把另一件旧事算在了我头上，只因为我的名字已经成了一个故事。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013710_

- **1947.08** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-declined` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef Huber正要把空床许给Berta Huber，Anton Berg却在楼梯上拦住了，说这条街上去年有人招了生人合住，失过钥匙，惹出些麻烦。Josef Huber回家把铺盖重新叠好，回绝了Berta Huber，只让他白天来喝口热水。空床仍空着，房租还是从自己的工钱里补。
  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1947.08** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Franz Huber这周主动擦了黑板。Josef Huber把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 42 -> 45; mood 0.01999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013707_

- **1948.01** `oj18-porter.night::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/carried-last-letter` tags: hospital, porter, night, illness, work, odd_job, everyday, vienna
  一个快不行的老人让Josef Huber把一封信送到城北的Emil Moser手里。Josef Huber下夜班后坐了四站电车，找到那间公寓；Emil Moser读完信，没有哭，只把一罐咖啡粉塞给Josef Huber。回程时，Josef Huber在电车最后一排睡着了，坐过了三站。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; food; mood -0.020000000000000004; cash -1 (the tram fare to the north of the city（其中1克朗记入债务）); state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:832; knowledge “老人给女儿的信只有一页，女儿读完没有哭，给我一罐咖啡粉。”; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1948.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fined` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Friedrich Berg 3在第三份合同上停住了：同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是Josef Huber。罚款单当场开出，金额不算大，但足够让下一季度的办公用品全部改成最便宜的牌子。Josef Huber没有争辩，只在收据背面写了日期。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.04999999999999999; mood -0.06; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:833; flag +fined_after_audit; knowledge “同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是我的。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-135; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1948.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef Huber在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Otto Weiss 2拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

- **1949.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Otto Weiss 2嫌Josef Huber的床单滴水滴到Otto Weiss 2家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Josef Huber下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Josef Huber把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Otto Weiss 2那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_

  Josef Winter在签字前把一张剧院包厢的票根留在桌上，说是“给孩子的”。Josef Huber把票根推回去，却在许可证上签了名；排水图仍缺一页，但窗口当天就盖了章。扩建开工后，第一场雨把后院淹了半尺。

- **1949.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/overwhelmed` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Josef Huber在整理旧衣服时突然停下来。那天剩下的活没有做完。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; state_parameter grief.years 0 -> 1_

- **1949.01** `r13-fam.look-for-lost-relative::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/wrong-person` tags: task, family, missing, official, war, search, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Bauer说名单上有一个同姓的人，住在城北的收容站。Helene Kral连夜赶去，见到的却是一个比她儿子年轻的裁缝。Josef Huber陪她走回来，路上Helene Kral一直说名字没有错，是年份错了；Josef Huber没有纠正，只把她的胳膊挽得更紧。
  _effects: cash -1 (念错的名字); mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +followed_a_false_lead; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126; knowledge “the name on the list belongs to another family”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

- **1949.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Josef Huber听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 25 -> 33; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 41 -> 38; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015253_

- **1949.04** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Berta Huber这周主动擦了黑板。Josef Huber把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 22 -> 25; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015070_

- **1949.07** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Hermann Winter 2把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Josef Huber去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1949.11** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-seal-was-used` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Otto Berg 2用Josef Huber留在抽屉里的印章签了一份采购单，把库房里最后一批煤卖给了城外的砖厂。Josef Huber发现时，账已经做平，签字栏里是Josef Huber自己的名字。Otto Berg 2说只是替Josef Huber省了麻烦，还把那支用旧的印泥放回了原处。
  _effects: cash -3 (为平账先垫出去的钱（其中3克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.10000000000000003; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; condition; flag +seal_used_by_trusted_hand; mood -0.07_

- **1949.11** `r2-faith.temperance-pledge::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/refused-the-pledge` tags: faith, temperance, tavern, pledge, reform, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef Huber把誓词推回去，说这条街上只有酒馆肯赊账给失业的人。Anna Weiss 3把签名簿合上，说那就别怪主日讲道提你的名字。Otto Berg 2在角落里鼓掌，圣马克斯殡葬合作社当天下午就送来一桶新酒，桶盖上用粉笔画了个十字。
  _effects: cash 3 (戒酒会上的第十个签名); reputation -0.030000000000000027; flag +refused_temperance_pledge; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; knowledge “the tavern extends credit to unemployed workers”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

  Josef Huber带着Anton Huber去学校登记。孩子的名字被写在名册最后一行，墨水还没有干；回家的路上，Anton Huber一直捏着那张课程表，Josef Huber则在算这个月还能剩下多少煤钱。

  Josef Huber把Helene Huber的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Helene Huber今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Josef Huber听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Josef Huber的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Josef Huber想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 38 -> 30; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 60 -> 55; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: state_parameter grief.years 1 -> 2; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Josef Huber想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1950.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef Huber记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Anna Huber说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Anna Huber陪着Josef Huber去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003191:child; mood -0.09999999999999998_

  市政厅要在这一片修新路，整排房子限期拆除。Hedwig说补偿按房间数算，Josef Huber家只有一间半，拿到的钱不够在附近租同样大的屋子。Josef Huber在拆迁协议上签字，用补偿款买了去城外的车票，回头看了三次。
  _effects: cash 10 (封条贴在自家门上); condition; flag +home_demolished_for_road; move place-wien_

- **1950.12** `r2-leisure.roulette::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/won-at-roulette` tags: leisure, casino, roulette, gambling, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  小球在轮盘上跳了三次，最后落进红格。Otto Kral 2把一叠筹码推过来，Anna Huber抓住Josef Huber的胳膊，说见好就收。Josef Huber又押了一次红，又赢了。第三次，Josef Huber把筹码收进口袋，说今晚够了。Anna Huber在门口数了数，够付半年房租。
  _effects: cash 25 (轮盘停在零); mood 0.15000000000000002; flag +won_at_roulette; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003191:child; knowledge “red came up three times and the croupier did not blink”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147_

  报纸在头版登了Josef Huber和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Josef Huber把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.030000000000000027; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:926; flag +scandal_weathered; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; flag +office_under_siege_

  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Josef Huber胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.10** `oj18-lift.stuck::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hand-crank` tags: lift, building, work, night, passenger, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  电梯停在四楼半，门缝里只看见一截电线。Josef Huber让Friedrich Kraus抓紧扶手，自己摇动手柄，一格一格把轿厢放下来。门开时，Friedrich Kraus的白大褂后背全湿了；他塞给Josef Huber两张钞票，说手术室里还有人等着。
  _effects: organization_found 环城大道写字楼管理处 (other); position 电梯员 @building:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2-evt:world-vienna-1912:016115; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:928; cash 2 (the doctor's reward); reputation 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001268:child; flag +lift_operator; knowledge “电梯停在四楼半时，手动柄要一格一格放，不能一次松到底。”_

- **1951.11** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/correct-it-twice` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  Josef Huber在窗口要求更正，Elisabeth Bauer把表格翻了三遍，最后开出一张新的出生证明，名字却少了一个字母。于是城里同时住着一个死人、一个活人，和一个只在档案里出生的陌生人。Josef Huber拿着两张纸站在区公所门口，忽然不知道该用哪一张去领下个月的煤票。
  _effects: cash -1 (更正材料的手续费和车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:927; relation rival with person-elisabeth-bauer; condition; flag +two_living_files_one_death; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Josef Huber把Anna Huber的名字写在“联络员”后面，月薪一克朗，职责是每周来两次、把外面的消息带进来。Hedwig说这个岗位没有编制，Josef Huber说先按临时用工算；第一个月，Anna Huber带来的消息比文件还多。
  _effects: position 联络员 @building:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2-evt:world-vienna-1912:016115; organization_funds; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003191:child; knowledge “联络员不在正式编制里，工资走临时用工。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Josef Huber被叫到学校，老师说Berta Huber这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Josef Huber没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 36 -> 31; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 69 -> 74; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:office-under-siege/pressure-year/new-allegation` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  第二天的报纸又登了一封匿名信，说Josef Huber把一份合同给了亲戚。信里没有证据，但足够让两个原本答应到场的人临时出差。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 30 -> 40; state_parameter office-under-siege.support 60 -> 54; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; reputation -0.02999999999999997; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Josef Huber的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Josef Huber收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Josef Huber先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 40 -> 47; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 15 -> 25; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Josef Huber的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Josef Huber想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 22; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 60 -> 55; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-gives-up` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef Huber在柜台前站了很久，最后把处方留在那里，说等有货再来。回家路上经过医院，看见药房窗口也挂着“无货”；第二天，处方被雨水泡烂在口袋里。Josef Huber把湿透的处方摊在炉边烤，字还是糊了。
  _effects: cash -1 (tram fare and a paper envelope for the prescription（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; role; knowledge “医院药房也会挂“无货”；处方留在柜台，第二天下雨就泡烂了。”_

- **1952.03** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Anton Huber这周主动擦了黑板。Josef Huber把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 33 -> 36; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017064_

- **1952.04** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef Huber只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Ferdinand Berg手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Ferdinand Berg把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Josef Huber记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_
