- **1953.01** `bc18-ghost.quiet-correction::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/school-inspector` tags: bureaucracy, paperwork, identity, absurd, legal, quiet, comic, vienna
  学务督察按着新户籍找上门，说有一名适龄儿童从未入学。Josef Huber躲在阁楼里，Friedrich Kraus在楼下说孩子出疹子，不能见风；督察留下了一张入学通知和一瓶鱼肝油。晚上，Josef Huber从阁楼下来，看见通知上写着“七岁”，忽然觉得自己这一生确实该重新开始。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 25 -> 40; reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001268:child; condition; flag +school_notice_for_newborn; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `state_reaction:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/another-file-opened` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Josef Huber刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Josef Huber站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 40 -> 52; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 2 -> 3; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017536_

- **1953.01** `r3-war.armistice-eleven::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::first-armistice-day/told-armistice-story` tags: war, armistice, ceasefire, aftermath, memory, vienna, urban, postwar
  纪念仪式上，Berta Huber问Josef Huber当年停战时在做什么。Josef Huber说在擦枪，擦到一半听见外面安静了，还以为是耳朵坏了。Berta Huber笑，Josef Huber也笑，笑完把手里那支没点的烟放回口袋。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +told_armistice_story_to_neighbor; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1953.01** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef Huber看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Ferdinand Berg追出来问怎么不领了，Josef Huber说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Josef Huber把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; food_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Josef Huber学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Josef Huber的材料齐全。年底，Josef Huber从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 47 -> 51; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 52 -> 47; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  一年过去，Josef Huber已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Josef Huber也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 22 -> 27; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 15; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 12; cash 1 (night-shift wages); skill_

  Josef Huber用三个月把账本上的零碎支出并成四类，又停掉两份没有人看的简报。年底结账时，Berta Huber发现不仅没有赤字，还剩下够换一批档案柜的钱；没有剪彩，只有仓库里新柜子的木头味。

  守灵夜设在死者生前常去的酒馆后间。Josef Huber推门进去，看见自己的照片立在蜡烛中间，Anna Weiss正用手帕擦眼角。Grete Winter 3问来客是谁，Josef Huber说“一个远亲”；话音落下，Anna Weiss抬起头，手帕掉在地上。半条街的人都来看热闹，Josef Huber被推到椅子上，稀里糊涂成了这场葬礼的主角。
  _effects: cash 1 (看热闹的人塞来的酒钱); reputation 0.020000000000000018; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:1055; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 47 -> 59; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 3 -> 4; condition; flag +attended_own_wake; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.10** `statistical_infection`
  Josef Huber在Wien病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1954.11** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef Huber从肺结核中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1954.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  夜里疼醒时，Josef Huber摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Berta Huber第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Josef Huber只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。

- **1955.11** `p9-history-medical-daily.house-call/carry-child-hospital` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily, injury
  Josef Huber用毯子裹住Maria Winter，搀着往综合医院急诊走，走到半路干脆背起来。长椅上挤满人，等到窗户发白，Maria Winter的烧退了些，Josef Huber的胳膊却抖得端不住水杯；雪灌进后领，鞋底磨得只剩一层布，这一夜把两个人的人情结结实实绑在了一起。
  _effects: health -0.04000000000000001 (雪夜背孩子去医院，肩臂脱力); mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:007105:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-170_

  Josef Huber把市政厅新贴的租金告示抄在一张纸上，带给Berta Huber看。Berta Huber起初说那是给新房客的，Josef Huber便指着告示上的日期，说不许涨。两人在门口争了半个钟头，最后按旧租收，收据上多盖了一个章。
  _effects: cash -3 (房东的收租日（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +invoked_rent_control; knowledge “the new rent notice applies to sitting tenants”_

- **1956.03** `af17-luck.wallet-in-the-snow::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-wallet-belonged-to-the-police` tags: luck, money, street, chance, irony, daily, vienna, urban
  Josef Huber刚把钱包交给街角岗亭，Franz Weiss就翻了翻里面的纸，问“你住哪条街”。钱包确实是失物，但失主是警局的人；Josef Huber拿回一张收据，回家路上总觉得有人在后面跟着。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000018; flag +returned_wallet_to_police; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Mitzi Weiss把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Josef Huber打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: health -0.03999999999999998 (高烧硬熬伤身); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002799:child_

- **1956.08** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef Huber把Rosa 2和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Josef Huber把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1957.01** `df18-ghost.funeral-coat::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-gravedigger` tags: ghost, grief, funeral, eerie, consoling, elderhood, supernatural, vienna
  Josef Huber朝铁栏外走过去，才发现那是一个穿着死者旧外套的掘墓人。他摘下帽子，说衣服是死者家里给的。两个人在墓园门口站了一会儿，Josef Huber把口袋里的一枚硬币给了他，请他给坟上多添一把土。
  _effects: cash -1 (请掘墓人添土的一枚硬币（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002799:child; knowledge “葬礼外的身影是一个穿着死者旧外套的掘墓人。”; flag +df18_ghost_was_gravedigger; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  一年过去，Josef Huber已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Josef Huber也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 14 -> 19; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 20 -> 25; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 12; cash 1 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1957.03** `kernel-health.chronic-pain::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pain-managed-at-home` tags: health, pain, elderhood, morphine, household, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef Huber把楼梯改成一步一步上，买菜只走平路，疼得厉害就坐下来揉。Mitzi Weiss替Josef Huber把煤筐拎上楼，换来一碗汤和一张旧报纸；两个人谁也没提医院。邻居把空筐靠在门边，顺手把楼梯上的冰敲掉。
  _effects: cash -4 (a cane, a hot-water bottle and tram fares to the market（其中4克朗记入债务）); health -0.010000000000000009 (chronic pain managed by rest and warmth); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002799:child; knowledge “膝盖疼的时候走平路、分几步上楼、用热水袋，比去医院的排队更管用。”_

- **1957.06** `em18-late-bloom.shop-sign::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-lease-is-refused` tags: elderhood, late_life, shop, craft, work, reinvention, neighborhood, vienna
  房东把Josef Huber的押金推回来，说这年纪要有担保人，还要一次付清半年房租。Josef Huber在门口站了一会儿，把写好的招牌尺寸纸折起来；回家路上，Josef Huber经过那扇空门面，里面已经挂上了成衣铺的布帘。
